
========== adminAuthRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");
const crypto = require("crypto");

const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

const ADMIN_COOKIE_NAME = "win.admin.sid";
const ADMIN_SESSION_HOURS = 8;
const MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const LOCKOUT_MINUTES = 15;

router.use((req, res, next) => {
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.setHeader("Pragma", "no-cache");
    next();
});

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function hashSessionToken(token) {
    return crypto
        .createHash("sha256")
        .update(token)
        .digest("hex");
}

function getAdminCookie(req) {
    const cookieHeader = req.headers.cookie || "";

    for (const part of cookieHeader.split(";")) {
        const separator = part.indexOf("=");

        if (separator === -1) continue;

        const name = part.slice(0, separator).trim();
        const value = part.slice(separator + 1).trim();

        if (name !== ADMIN_COOKIE_NAME) continue;

        try {
            return decodeURIComponent(value);
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function clearAdminCookie(res) {
    res.clearCookie(ADMIN_COOKIE_NAME, {
        httpOnly: true,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
        sameSite: "strict",
        path: "/"
    });
}

function setAdminCookie(res, token) {
    res.cookie(ADMIN_COOKIE_NAME, token, {
        httpOnly: true,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
        sameSite: "strict",
        path: "/",
        maxAge: ADMIN_SESSION_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000
    });
}

async function recordSecurityEvent(
    adminId,
    email,
    eventType,
    req
) {
    await run(
        `INSERT INTO admin_security_events (
            admin_user_id,
            email,
            event_type,
            ip_address,
            user_agent
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            adminId || null,
            email || null,
            eventType,
            req.ip || null,
            req.get("user-agent") || null
        ]
    );
}

async function recordActivity(
    adminId,
    action,
    targetType,
    targetId,
    details,
    req
) {
    await run(
        `INSERT INTO admin_activity_logs (
            admin_user_id,
            action,
            target_type,
            target_id,
            details_json,
            ip_address,
            user_agent
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            adminId,
            action,
            targetType || null,
            targetId || null,
            JSON.stringify(details || {}),
            req.ip || null,
            req.get("user-agent") || null
        ]
    );
}

function invalidLoginResponse(res) {
    return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid administrator email or password."
    });
}

// ADMIN LOGIN
router.post("/login", async (req, res) => {
    const email =
        typeof req.body?.email === "string"
            ? req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase()
            : "";

    const password =
        typeof req.body?.password === "string"
            ? req.body.password
            : "";

    if (
        !email ||
        email.length > 254 ||
        !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ||
        !password ||
        Buffer.byteLength(password, "utf8") > 72
    ) {
        return invalidLoginResponse(res);
    }

    try {
        const admin = await get(
            `SELECT
                id,
                full_name,
                email,
                password_hash,
                role,
                status,
                failed_login_attempts,
                locked_until,
                CASE
                    WHEN locked_until IS NOT NULL
                     AND locked_until > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                    THEN 1
                    ELSE 0
                END AS is_locked
             FROM admin_users
             WHERE email = ?
             LIMIT 1`,
            [email]
        );

        if (!admin) {
            await recordSecurityEvent(
                null,
                email,
                "admin_login_failed",
                req
            );

            return invalidLoginResponse(res);
        }

        if (admin.status !== "active") {
            await recordSecurityEvent(
                admin.id,
                admin.email,
                "admin_login_blocked_inactive",
                req
            );

            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This administrator account is unavailable."
            });
        }

        if (Number(admin.is_locked) === 1) {
            await recordSecurityEvent(
                admin.id,
                admin.email,
                "admin_login_blocked_locked",
                req
            );

            return res.status(429).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Too many unsuccessful attempts. Please try again later."
            });
        }

        const passwordMatches = await bcrypt.compare(
            password,
            admin.password_hash
        );

        if (!passwordMatches) {
            await run(
                `UPDATE admin_users
                 SET
                    failed_login_attempts =
                        CASE
                            WHEN locked_until IS NOT NULL
                             AND locked_until <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                            THEN 1
                            ELSE failed_login_attempts + 1
                        END,

                    locked_until =
                        CASE
                            WHEN (
                                CASE
                                    WHEN locked_until IS NOT NULL
                                     AND locked_until <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                                    THEN 1
                                    ELSE failed_login_attempts + 1
                                END
                            ) >= ?
                            THEN datetime('now', '+15 minutes')
                            ELSE NULL
                        END,

                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = ?`,
                [MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS, admin.id]
            );

            const updatedAdmin = await get(
                `SELECT failed_login_attempts, locked_until
                 FROM admin_users
                 WHERE id = ?`,
                [admin.id]
            );

            const locked =
                updatedAdmin &&
                updatedAdmin.locked_until !== null &&
                updatedAdmin.failed_login_attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS;

            await recordSecurityEvent(
                admin.id,
                admin.email,
                locked
                    ? "admin_account_temporarily_locked"
                    : "admin_login_failed",
                req
            );

            if (locked) {
                return res.status(429).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Too many unsuccessful attempts. Your account is temporarily locked for 15 minutes."
                });
            }

            return invalidLoginResponse(res);
        }

        const rawToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
        const sessionHash = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

        const previousToken = getAdminCookie(req);
        const previousSessionHash =
            previousToken &&
            /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(previousToken)
                ? hashSessionToken(previousToken)
                : null;

        await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

        try {
            // Revoke a previous administrator session in this browser.
            if (previousSessionHash) {
                await run(
                    `UPDATE admin_sessions
                     SET revoked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE session_id_hash = ?
                       AND revoked_at IS NULL`,
                    [previousSessionHash]
                );
            }

            // Store only the hash of the browser's session token.
            await run(
                `INSERT INTO admin_sessions (
                    session_id_hash,
                    admin_user_id,
                    expires_at,
                    ip_address,
                    user_agent
                ) VALUES (
                    ?,
                    ?,
                    datetime('now', '+8 hours'),
                    ?,
                    ?
                )`,
                [
                    sessionHash,
                    admin.id,
                    req.ip || null,
                    req.get("user-agent") || null
                ]
            );

            await run(
                `UPDATE admin_users
                 SET
                    failed_login_attempts = 0,
                    locked_until = NULL,
                    last_login_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    last_login_ip = ?,
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = ?`,
                [req.ip || null, admin.id]
            );

            await recordSecurityEvent(
                admin.id,
                admin.email,
                "admin_login_success",
                req
            );

            await recordActivity(
                admin.id,
                "admin_login",
                "admin_user",
                String(admin.id),
                { role: admin.role },
                req
            );

            await run("COMMIT");

        } catch (transactionError) {
            try {
                await run("ROLLBACK");
            } catch (rollbackError) {
                console.error(
                    "Admin login rollback error:",
                    rollbackError.message
                );
            }

            throw transactionError;
        }

        // Issue the separate administrator cookie only after
        // the database session has been saved successfully.
        setAdminCookie(res, rawToken);

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Administrator login successful.",
            admin: {
                id: admin.id,
                full_name: admin.full_name,
                email: admin.email,
                role: admin.role
            },
            redirect: "/admin/dashboard.html"
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Admin login error:", error.message);

        clearAdminCookie(res);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Administrator login could not be completed."
        });
    }
});

// GET CURRENT ADMINISTRATOR
router.get("/me", requireAdminAuth, (req, res) => {
    return res.json({
        success: true,
        admin: req.adminUser,
        permissions: [...req.adminPermissions].sort()
    });
});

// ADMIN LOGOUT
router.post("/logout", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const rawToken = getAdminCookie(req);

        if (
            typeof rawToken === "string" &&
            /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(rawToken)
        ) {
            const sessionHash = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

            const session = await get(
                `SELECT admin_user_id
                 FROM admin_sessions
                 WHERE session_id_hash = ?
                   AND revoked_at IS NULL`,
                [sessionHash]
            );

            if (session) {
                await run(
                    `UPDATE admin_sessions
                     SET revoked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE session_id_hash = ?`,
                    [sessionHash]
                );

                await recordSecurityEvent(
                    session.admin_user_id,
                    null,
                    "admin_logout",
                    req
                );

                await recordActivity(
                    session.admin_user_id,
                    "admin_logout",
                    "admin_user",
                    String(session.admin_user_id),
                    {},
                    req
                );
            }
        }

        clearAdminCookie(res);

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Administrator logged out successfully."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Admin logout error:", error.message);

        clearAdminCookie(res);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not complete administrator logout."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== adminCustomersRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAdminAuth);

// ==========================================
// DATABASE HELPERS
// ==========================================

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function parsePositiveInteger(value, fallback, maximum) {
    if (value === undefined) return fallback;

    const parsed = Number(value);

    if (
        !Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) ||
        parsed < 1 ||
        parsed > maximum
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return parsed;
}

function escapeLike(value) {
    return value.replace(/[\\%_]/g, "\\$&");
}

// Safely encode text values for the transaction SQL script.
// Single quotes are escaped using SQLite's SQL string-literal rules.
function sqlLiteral(value) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
        return "NULL";
    }

    return `'${String(value).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
}

function buildSearch(search) {
    if (!search) {
        return {
            sql: "",
            params: []
        };
    }

    const pattern = `%${escapeLike(search)}%`;

    return {
        sql: `
            WHERE (
                businesses.business_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR businesses.business_type LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR businesses.email LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR businesses.phone LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR businesses.country LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR owner.full_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                OR owner.email LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
            )
        `,
        params: Array(7).fill(pattern)
    };
}

function hasPermission(req, permission) {
    return req.adminPermissions instanceof Set &&
        req.adminPermissions.has(permission);
}

function validateBusinessId(value) {
    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(value)
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const id = Number(value);

    return Number.isSafeInteger(id) ? id : null;
}

function validateReason(req, res) {
    const reason = req.body?.reason;

    if (typeof reason !== "string") {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "An action reason is required."
        });

        return null;
    }

    const cleanReason = reason.trim();

    if (cleanReason.length < 5 || cleanReason.length > 500) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The reason must contain between 5 and 500 characters."
        });

        return null;
    }

    return cleanReason;
}

// Execute the account update and audit insertion in one SQLite transaction.
function executeTransaction(sql) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.exec(sql, error => {
            if (!error) {
                return resolve();
            }

            db.exec("ROLLBACK", rollbackError => {
                if (rollbackError) {
                    console.error(
                        "Admin account transaction rollback failed:",
                        rollbackError.message
                    );
                }

                reject(error);
            });
        });
    });
}

// ==========================================
// BUSINESS STATUS CHANGE
// ==========================================

async function changeBusinessStatus(req, res, settings) {
    try {
        const {
            requiredPermission,
            fromStatus,
            toStatus,
            action
        } = settings;

        if (!hasPermission(req, requiredPermission)) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to perform this action."
            });
        }

        const businessId = validateBusinessId(
            req.params.businessId
        );

        if (!businessId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid business account ID."
            });
        }

        const reason = validateReason(req, res);

        if (!reason) return;

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

        if (
            !Number.isSafeInteger(adminId) ||
            adminId < 1
        ) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Administrator authentication is required."
            });
        }

        const business = await get(
            `SELECT id, business_name, account_status
             FROM businesses
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!business) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Business account not found."
            });
        }

        if (business.account_status !== fromStatus) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "BUSINESS_STATUS_CONFLICT",
                message: business.account_status === toStatus
                    ? `This business is already ${toStatus}.`
                    : "The business account status has changed. Refresh and try again.",
                accountStatus: business.account_status
            });
        }

        const details = JSON.stringify({
            businessName: business.business_name,
            businessId,
            previousStatus: fromStatus,
            newStatus: toStatus,
            reason
        });

        const ipAddress = req.ip || null;
        const userAgent = req.get("user-agent")
            ? req.get("user-agent").slice(0, 500)
            : null;

        let updateSql;

        if (toStatus === "suspended") {
            updateSql = `
                UPDATE businesses
                SET
                    account_status = 'suspended',
                    suspension_reason = ${sqlLiteral(reason)},
                    suspended_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    suspended_by_admin_id = ${adminId}
                WHERE
                    id = ${businessId}
                    AND account_status = 'active';
            `;
        } else {
            updateSql = `
                UPDATE businesses
                SET
                    account_status = 'active',
                    suspension_reason = NULL,
                    suspended_at = NULL,
                    suspended_by_admin_id = NULL
                WHERE
                    id = ${businessId}
                    AND account_status = 'suspended';
            `;
        }

        const auditSql = `
            INSERT INTO admin_activity_logs (
                admin_user_id,
                action,
                target_type,
                target_id,
                details_json,
                ip_address,
                user_agent
            )
            SELECT
                ${adminId},
                ${sqlLiteral(action)},
                'business',
                ${sqlLiteral(String(businessId))},
                ${sqlLiteral(details)},
                ${sqlLiteral(ipAddress)},
                ${sqlLiteral(userAgent)}
            WHERE changes() = 1;
        `;

        await executeTransaction(`
            BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
            ${updateSql}
            ${auditSql}
            COMMIT;
        `);

        const updatedBusiness = await get(
            `SELECT
                id,
                business_name,
                account_status,
                suspension_reason,
                suspended_at
             FROM businesses
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!updatedBusiness) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Business account was not found after the operation."
            });
        }

        if (updatedBusiness.account_status !== toStatus) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "BUSINESS_STATUS_CONFLICT",
                message: "The business status changed before the operation could be completed.",
                accountStatus: updatedBusiness.account_status
            });
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: toStatus === "suspended"
                ? "Business account suspended successfully."
                : "Business account restored successfully.",
            business: updatedBusiness
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Admin business status operation failed:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The business account status could not be changed."
        });
    }
}

// ==========================================
// GET REGISTERED BUSINESSES
// ==========================================

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "customers.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view customers."
            });
        }

        const page = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.page,
            1,
            1000000
        );

        const requestedLimit = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.limit,
            10,
            50
        );

        if (page === null || requestedLimit === null) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid pagination parameters."
            });
        }

        const allowedLimits = [10, 25, 50];

        if (!allowedLimits.includes(requestedLimit)) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Page size must be 10, 25 or 50."
            });
        }

        const limit = requestedLimit;
        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const rawSearch = req.query.search ?? "";

        if (typeof rawSearch !== "string") {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid search value."
            });
        }

        const search = rawSearch.trim();

        if (search.length > 100) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Search must not exceed 100 characters."
            });
        }

        const searchQuery = buildSearch(search);

        const ownerJoin = `
            LEFT JOIN users AS owner
                ON owner.id = (
                    SELECT account_owner.id
                    FROM users AS account_owner
                    WHERE account_owner.business_id = businesses.id
                    ORDER BY
                        CASE
                            WHEN account_owner.role = 'owner' THEN 0
                            ELSE 1
                        END,
                        account_owner.id ASC
                    LIMIT 1
                )
        `;

        const subscriptionJoin = `
            LEFT JOIN subscriptions AS subscription
                ON subscription.business_id = businesses.id
        `;

        const joins = `
            ${ownerJoin}
            ${subscriptionJoin}
        `;

        const countResult = await get(
            `
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
                FROM businesses
                ${joins}
                ${searchQuery.sql}
            `,
            searchQuery.params
        );

        const total = Number(countResult?.total || 0);

        const businesses = await all(
            `
                SELECT
                    businesses.id,
                    businesses.business_name,
                    businesses.business_type,
                    businesses.email,
                    businesses.phone,
                    businesses.country,
                    businesses.currency,
                    businesses.created_at,

                    businesses.account_status,
                    businesses.suspension_reason,
                    businesses.suspended_at,

                    owner.full_name AS owner_name,
                    owner.email AS owner_email,

                    subscription.plan_name AS subscription_plan,
                    subscription.status AS subscription_status,
                    subscription.trial_started_at,
                    subscription.trial_ends_at,
                    subscription.current_period_start,
                    subscription.current_period_end

                FROM businesses

                ${joins}

                ${searchQuery.sql}

                ORDER BY
                    businesses.created_at DESC,
                    businesses.id DESC

                LIMIT ?
                OFFSET ?
            `,
            [
                ...searchQuery.params,
                limit,
                offset
            ]
        );

        const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit);

        return res.json({
            success: true,

            businesses,

            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages,
                hasNext: page < totalPages,
                hasPrevious: page > 1
            },

            search,

            permissions: {
                canSuspend: hasPermission(req, "customers.suspend"),
                canRestore: hasPermission(req, "customers.restore")
            },

            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Admin Customers API error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not retrieve registered businesses."
        });
    }
});

// ==========================================
// SUSPEND BUSINESS
// ==========================================

router.post("/:businessId/suspend", async (req, res) => {
    return changeBusinessStatus(req, res, {
        requiredPermission: "customers.suspend",
        fromStatus: "active",
        toStatus: "suspended",
        action: "business.suspended"
    });
});

// ==========================================
// RESTORE BUSINESS
// ==========================================

router.post("/:businessId/restore", async (req, res) => {
    return changeBusinessStatus(req, res, {
        requiredPermission: "customers.restore",
        fromStatus: "suspended",
        toStatus: "active",
        action: "business.restored"
    });
});

module.exports = router;

========== adminMaintenanceRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAdminAuth);

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function hasPermission(req, permission) {
    return (
        req.adminPermissions instanceof Set &&
        req.adminPermissions.has(permission)
    );
}

function denyPermission(res) {
    return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: "You do not have permission to perform this action."
    });
}

function sendError(res, error, message) {
    console.error(message, error);

    return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message
    });
}

function sendValidationError(res, error) {
    return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: error.message
    });
}

function normalizeDateTime(value, fieldName) {
    if (value === null || value === "") {
        return null;
    }

    if (typeof value !== "string") {
        throw new Error(`${fieldName} must be a valid date and time.`);
    }

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        throw new Error(`${fieldName} must be a valid date and time.`);
    }

    return date.toISOString()
        .slice(0, 19)
        .replace("T", " ");
}

function readStoredDate(value) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== "string") {
        return null;
    }

    const date = new Date(`${value.replace(" ", "T")}Z`);

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
}

function validateText(value, fieldName, maxLength, allowEmpty = false) {
    if (typeof value !== "string") {
        throw new Error(`${fieldName} must be text.`);
    }

    const cleaned = value.trim();

    if (!allowEmpty && !cleaned) {
        throw new Error(`${fieldName} cannot be empty.`);
    }

    if (cleaned.length > maxLength) {
        throw new Error(
            `${fieldName} cannot exceed ${maxLength} characters.`
        );
    }

    return cleaned;
}

function getOptionalText(body, fieldName, maxLength, currentValue) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, fieldName)) {
        return currentValue;
    }

    if (body[fieldName] === null) {
        return null;
    }

    return validateText(
        body[fieldName],
        fieldName,
        maxLength,
        true
    );
}

function getOptionalDate(body, fieldName, currentValue) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, fieldName)) {
        return currentValue;
    }

    return normalizeDateTime(body[fieldName], fieldName);
}

async function getMaintenanceSettings() {
    return get(`
        SELECT *
        FROM platform_maintenance
        WHERE id = 1
        LIMIT 1
    `);
}

async function writeMaintenanceEvent({
    eventType,
    req = null,
    adminUserId = null,
    previousState = null,
    newState = null,
    details = null
}) {
    await run(
        `INSERT INTO maintenance_events (
            event_type,
            admin_user_id,
            previous_state_json,
            new_state_json,
            details
        )
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            eventType,
            adminUserId,
            previousState ? JSON.stringify(previousState) : null,
            newState ? JSON.stringify(newState) : null,
            details
        ]
    );

    if (adminUserId !== null) {
        await run(
            `INSERT INTO admin_activity_logs (
                admin_user_id,
                action,
                target_type,
                target_id,
                details_json,
                ip_address,
                user_agent
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            [
                adminUserId,
                eventType,
                "platform_maintenance",
                "1",
                JSON.stringify({
                    previousState,
                    newState,
                    details
                }),
                req ? req.ip : null,
                req ? req.get("user-agent") || null : null
            ]
        );
    }
}

// ==========================================
// PERSISTENT SCHEDULER FAILURE LOGGING
// ==========================================

async function getLatestSchedulerHealthEvent() {
    return get(
        `SELECT event_type, created_at
         FROM maintenance_events
         WHERE event_type IN (
             'maintenance.scheduler_failed',
             'maintenance.scheduler_recovered'
         )
         ORDER BY id DESC
         LIMIT 1`
    );
}

async function recordSchedulerFailure(errorMessage) {
    try {
        const latest = await getLatestSchedulerHealthEvent();

        if (latest && latest.event_type === "maintenance.scheduler_failed") {
            const failureDate = readStoredDate(latest.created_at);

            if (
                failureDate &&
                Date.now() - failureDate.getTime() < 5 * 60 * 1000
            ) {
                return;
            }
        }

        await writeMaintenanceEvent({
            eventType: "maintenance.scheduler_failed",
            details: `Automatic maintenance scheduler failed: ${String(
                errorMessage
            ).slice(0, 2000)}`
        });

        console.error(
            "WIN automatic maintenance failure was recorded in maintenance history."
        );
    } catch (loggingError) {
        console.error(
            "Could not persist the maintenance scheduler failure:",
            loggingError.message
        );
    }
}

async function recordSchedulerRecovery() {
    try {
        const latest = await getLatestSchedulerHealthEvent();

        if (!latest || latest.event_type !== "maintenance.scheduler_failed") {
            return;
        }

        await writeMaintenanceEvent({
            eventType: "maintenance.scheduler_recovered",
            details: "The automatic maintenance scheduler is operating successfully again."
        });

        console.log(
            "WIN automatic maintenance scheduler recovery was recorded."
        );
    } catch (loggingError) {
        console.error(
            "Could not persist the maintenance scheduler recovery:",
            loggingError.message
        );
    }
}

async function withTransaction(callback) {
    await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

    try {
        const result = await callback();
        await run("COMMIT");
        return result;
    } catch (error) {
        try {
            await run("ROLLBACK");
        } catch (rollbackError) {
            console.error(
                "Maintenance transaction rollback failed:",
                rollbackError.message
            );
        }

        throw error;
    }
}

function getPublicStatus(settings) {
    if (Number(settings.maintenance_enabled) === 1) {
        return "under_maintenance";
    }

    if (Number(settings.schedule_enabled) === 1) {
        return "scheduled";
    }

    return "operational";
}

function requireBodyObject(req) {
    return (
        req.body &&
        typeof req.body === "object" &&
        !Array.isArray(req.body)
    );
}

// ==========================================
// GET CURRENT MAINTENANCE STATUS
// ==========================================

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.view")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    try {
        const settings = await getMaintenanceSettings();

        if (!settings) {
            return res.status(503).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Maintenance settings are unavailable."
            });
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            status: getPublicStatus(settings),
            settings
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not load maintenance status."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// GET MAINTENANCE EVENT HISTORY
// ==========================================

router.get("/history", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.view")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    try {
        const events = await all(
            `SELECT
                events.id,
                events.event_type,
                events.admin_user_id,
                events.previous_state_json,
                events.new_state_json,
                events.details,
                events.created_at,
                admins.full_name AS admin_name,
                admins.email AS admin_email
             FROM maintenance_events AS events
             LEFT JOIN admin_users AS admins
                ON admins.id = events.admin_user_id
             ORDER BY events.id DESC
             LIMIT 100`
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            events
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not load maintenance history."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// UPDATE MAINTENANCE MESSAGE AND SETTINGS
// ==========================================

router.patch("/settings", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.manage")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    if (!requireBodyObject(req)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "A valid settings object is required."
        });
    }

    try {
        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const previous = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!previous) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance settings are unavailable.");
            }

            const message = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "message",
                1000,
                previous.message
            );

            if (!message) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance message cannot be empty.");
            }

            const reason = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "reason",
                500,
                previous.reason
            );

            const expectedReturnAt = getOptionalDate(
                req.body,
                "expectedReturnAt",
                previous.expected_return_at
            );

            await run(
                `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                 SET message = ?,
                     reason = ?,
                     expected_return_at = ?,
                     last_changed_by = ?,
                     updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = 1`,
                [
                    message,
                    reason,
                    expectedReturnAt,
                    req.adminUser.id
                ]
            );

            const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                eventType: "maintenance.settings_updated",
                req,
                adminUserId: req.adminUser.id,
                previousState: previous,
                newState: updated,
                details: "Maintenance message or settings updated."
            });

            return updated;
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Maintenance settings updated.",
            settings: result
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (
            error.message &&
            (
                error.message.includes("must be") ||
                error.message.includes("cannot")
            )
        ) {
            return sendValidationError(res, error);
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not update maintenance settings."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// ACTIVATE MAINTENANCE IMMEDIATELY
// ==========================================

router.post("/activate", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.manage")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    if (!requireBodyObject(req)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "A valid activation request is required."
        });
    }

    try {
        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const previous = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!previous) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance settings are unavailable.");
            }

            if (Number(previous.maintenance_enabled) === 1) {
                const error = new Error("Maintenance is already active.");
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            const message = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "message",
                1000,
                previous.message
            );

            if (!message) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance message cannot be empty.");
            }

            const reason = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "reason",
                500,
                previous.reason
            );

            const expectedReturnAt = getOptionalDate(
                req.body,
                "expectedReturnAt",
                previous.expected_return_at
            );

            await run(
                `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                 SET maintenance_enabled = 1,
                     message = ?,
                     reason = ?,
                     expected_return_at = ?,
                     schedule_enabled = 0,
                     scheduled_start_at = NULL,
                     scheduled_end_at = NULL,
                     activated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                     last_changed_by = ?,
                     updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = 1`,
                [
                    message,
                    reason,
                    expectedReturnAt,
                    req.adminUser.id
                ]
            );

            const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                eventType: "maintenance.activated",
                req,
                adminUserId: req.adminUser.id,
                previousState: previous,
                newState: updated,
                details: "Maintenance activated manually."
            });

            return updated;
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Maintenance activated.",
            status: "under_maintenance",
            settings: result
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) {
            return res.status(error.statusCode).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        if (
            error.message &&
            (
                error.message.includes("must be") ||
                error.message.includes("cannot")
            )
        ) {
            return sendValidationError(res, error);
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not activate maintenance."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// RESTORE CUSTOMER ACCESS IMMEDIATELY
// ==========================================

router.post("/deactivate", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.manage")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    try {
        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const previous = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!previous) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance settings are unavailable.");
            }

            if (Number(previous.maintenance_enabled) !== 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "Maintenance is not currently active."
                );
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            await run(
                `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                 SET maintenance_enabled = 0,
                     schedule_enabled = 0,
                     scheduled_start_at = NULL,
                     scheduled_end_at = NULL,
                     activated_at = NULL,
                     last_changed_by = ?,
                     updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = 1`,
                [req.adminUser.id]
            );

            const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                eventType: "maintenance.deactivated",
                req,
                adminUserId: req.adminUser.id,
                previousState: previous,
                newState: updated,
                details: "Maintenance ended manually. Customer access restored."
            });

            return updated;
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Maintenance ended. Customer access restored.",
            status: "operational",
            settings: result
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) {
            return res.status(error.statusCode).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not end maintenance."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// SCHEDULE FUTURE MAINTENANCE
// ==========================================

router.post("/schedule", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.manage")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    if (!requireBodyObject(req)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "A valid schedule request is required."
        });
    }

    try {
        const startAt = normalizeDateTime(
            req.body.startAt,
            "Start date and time"
        );

        const endAt = normalizeDateTime(
            req.body.endAt,
            "End date and time"
        );

        if (!startAt || !endAt) {
            throw new Error(
                "Both maintenance start and end times are required."
            );
        }

        const startDate = readStoredDate(startAt);
        const endDate = readStoredDate(endAt);

        if (!startDate || !endDate || endDate <= startDate) {
            throw new Error(
                "The maintenance end time must be after its start time."
            );
        }

        if (startDate <= new Date()) {
            throw new Error(
                "The maintenance start time must be in the future."
            );
        }

        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const previous = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!previous) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance settings are unavailable.");
            }

            if (Number(previous.maintenance_enabled) === 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "End the current maintenance before scheduling another window."
                );
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            const message = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "message",
                1000,
                previous.message
            );

            if (!message) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance message cannot be empty.");
            }

            const reason = getOptionalText(
                req.body,
                "reason",
                500,
                previous.reason
            );

            const expectedReturnAt = getOptionalDate(
                req.body,
                "expectedReturnAt",
                endAt
            );

            await run(
                `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                 SET maintenance_enabled = 0,
                     message = ?,
                     reason = ?,
                     expected_return_at = ?,
                     schedule_enabled = 1,
                     scheduled_start_at = ?,
                     scheduled_end_at = ?,
                     activated_at = NULL,
                     last_changed_by = ?,
                     updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = 1`,
                [
                    message,
                    reason,
                    expectedReturnAt || endAt,
                    startAt,
                    endAt,
                    req.adminUser.id
                ]
            );

            const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                eventType: "maintenance.scheduled",
                req,
                adminUserId: req.adminUser.id,
                previousState: previous,
                newState: updated,
                details: "A future maintenance window was scheduled."
            });

            return updated;
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Maintenance window scheduled.",
            status: "scheduled",
            settings: result
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) {
            return res.status(error.statusCode).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        if (
            error.message &&
            (
                error.message.includes("must be") ||
                error.message.includes("required") ||
                error.message.includes("cannot")
            )
        ) {
            return sendValidationError(res, error);
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not schedule maintenance."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// CANCEL A FUTURE MAINTENANCE WINDOW
// ==========================================

router.post("/schedule/cancel", async (req, res) => {
    if (!hasPermission(req, "maintenance.manage")) {
        return denyPermission(res);
    }

    try {
        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const previous = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!previous) {
                throw new Error("Maintenance settings are unavailable.");
            }

            if (Number(previous.schedule_enabled) !== 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "There is no scheduled maintenance window to cancel."
                );
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            if (Number(previous.maintenance_enabled) === 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "Maintenance has already started. Use End Maintenance to restore customer access."
                );
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            const scheduledStart = readStoredDate(
                previous.scheduled_start_at
            );

            if (!scheduledStart) {
                throw new Error(
                    "The scheduled start time is invalid. Review the maintenance settings."
                );
            }

            if (scheduledStart <= new Date()) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "The scheduled start time has arrived. Wait for the automatic processor, then use End Maintenance if necessary."
                );
                error.statusCode = 409;
                throw error;
            }

            await run(
                `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                 SET schedule_enabled = 0,
                     scheduled_start_at = NULL,
                     scheduled_end_at = NULL,
                     last_changed_by = ?,
                     updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = 1
                   AND schedule_enabled = 1
                   AND maintenance_enabled = 0`,
                [req.adminUser.id]
            );

            const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                eventType: "maintenance.schedule_cancelled",
                req,
                adminUserId: req.adminUser.id,
                previousState: previous,
                newState: updated,
                details: "Future maintenance window cancelled."
            });

            return updated;
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Scheduled maintenance cancelled.",
            status: "operational",
            settings: result
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) {
            return res.status(error.statusCode).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        if (error.message && error.message.includes("invalid")) {
            return sendValidationError(res, error);
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not cancel maintenance schedule."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// AUTOMATIC MAINTENANCE SCHEDULER
// ==========================================

async function processDueMaintenance() {
    try {
        const result = await withTransaction(async () => {
            const settings = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (!settings) {
                return {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Maintenance settings are unavailable."
                };
            }

            if (Number(settings.schedule_enabled) !== 1) {
                return {
                    success: true,
                    processed: false
                };
            }

            const now = new Date();

            const startDate = readStoredDate(
                settings.scheduled_start_at
            );

            const endDate = readStoredDate(
                settings.scheduled_end_at
            );

            let changed = false;

            if (
                Number(settings.maintenance_enabled) !== 1 &&
                startDate &&
                startDate <= now
            ) {
                const previous = settings;

                await run(
                    `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                     SET maintenance_enabled = 1,
                         activated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                         updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE id = 1
                       AND schedule_enabled = 1
                       AND maintenance_enabled = 0`
                );

                const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

                await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                    eventType: "maintenance.activated_automatically",
                    previousState: previous,
                    newState: updated,
                    details: "Scheduled maintenance started automatically."
                });

                changed = true;
            }

            const latest = await getMaintenanceSettings();

            if (
                latest &&
                Number(latest.maintenance_enabled) === 1 &&
                endDate &&
                endDate <= now
            ) {
                const previous = latest;

                await run(
                    `UPDATE platform_maintenance
                     SET maintenance_enabled = 0,
                         schedule_enabled = 0,
                         scheduled_start_at = NULL,
                         scheduled_end_at = NULL,
                         activated_at = NULL,
                         updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE id = 1
                       AND schedule_enabled = 1
                       AND maintenance_enabled = 1`
                );

                const updated = await getMaintenanceSettings();

                await writeMaintenanceEvent({
                    eventType: "maintenance.deactivated_automatically",
                    previousState: previous,
                    newState: updated,
                    details: "Scheduled maintenance ended automatically. Customer access restored."
                });

                changed = true;
            }

            return {
                success: true,
                processed: changed
            };
        });

        if (result.success) {
            await recordSchedulerRecovery();
        } else {
            await recordSchedulerFailure(result.message);
        }

        return result;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "WIN automatic maintenance processor failed:",
            error.message
        );

        await recordSchedulerFailure(error.message);

        return {
            success: false,
            message: error.message
        };
    }
}

module.exports = router;
module.exports.processDueMaintenance = processDueMaintenance;

========== adminNotificationsRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAdminAuth);

const CATEGORIES = {
    maintenance: {
        display: "maintenance",
        database: "maintenance",
        notification: "maintenance"
    },
    announcements: {
        display: "announcements",
        database: "platform_announcement",
        notification: "platform_announcement"
    },
    welcome: {
        display: "welcome",
        database: "general",
        notification: "welcome"
    },
    subscription: {
        display: "subscription",
        database: "subscription",
        notification: "subscription"
    },
    general: {
        display: "general",
        database: "general",
        notification: "general"
    }
};

const AUDIENCES = [
    "all",
    "trial",
    "active",
    "pending",
    "selected_users",
    "business_type"
];

// ==========================================
// DATABASE HELPERS
// ==========================================

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function hasPermission(req, permission) {
    return req.adminPermissions instanceof Set &&
        req.adminPermissions.has(permission);
}

function parsePositiveInteger(value, fallback, maximum) {
    if (value === undefined) return fallback;

    const parsed = Number(value);

    if (
        !Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) ||
        parsed < 1 ||
        parsed > maximum
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return parsed;
}

function validateId(value) {
    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(value)
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const id = Number(value);
    return Number.isSafeInteger(id) ? id : null;
}

function getRequestMetadata(req) {
    return {
        ipAddress: req?.ip || null,
        userAgent: req?.get("user-agent")
            ? req.get("user-agent").slice(0, 500)
            : null
    };
}

function sendError(res, error, message) {
    console.error(
        "Admin Notifications API error:",
        error.message
    );

    return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message
    });
}

// ==========================================
// TRANSACTION HELPER
// ==========================================

function executeTransaction(sql) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.exec(sql, error => {
            if (!error) return resolve();

            db.exec("ROLLBACK", rollbackError => {
                if (rollbackError) {
                    console.error(
                        "Admin notification rollback failed:",
                        rollbackError.message
                    );
                }

                reject(error);
            });
        });
    });
}

// ==========================================
// INPUT VALIDATION
// ==========================================

function parseBoolean(value, fallback = false) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
        return fallback;
    }

    if (value === true || value === 1 || value === "1") {
        return true;
    }

    if (value === false || value === 0 || value === "0") {
        return false;
    }

    return null;
}

function validateDate(value, fieldName, res) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
        return null;
    }

    if (typeof value !== "string") {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: `Invalid ${fieldName}.`
        });

        return undefined;
    }

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: `Invalid ${fieldName}.`
        });

        return undefined;
    }

    return date.toISOString();
}

function validateCampaign(body, res) {
    const category = CATEGORIES[body.category];

    if (!category) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Choose one of the five supported notification categories."
        });

        return null;
    }

    if (
        typeof body.title !== "string" ||
        body.title.trim().length < 3 ||
        body.title.trim().length > 100
    ) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The title must contain between 3 and 100 characters."
        });

        return null;
    }

    if (
        typeof body.message !== "string" ||
        body.message.trim().length < 5 ||
        body.message.trim().length > 2000
    ) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The message must contain between 5 and 2,000 characters."
        });

        return null;
    }

    const audienceType = body.audienceType || "all";

    if (!AUDIENCES.includes(audienceType)) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid notification audience."
        });

        return null;
    }

    let audienceValue = null;

    if (audienceType === "selected_users") {
        let ids = body.selectedUserIds;

        if (
            !Array.isArray(ids) &&
            typeof body.audienceValue === "string"
        ) {
            try {
                ids = JSON.parse(body.audienceValue);
            } catch {
                ids = null;
            }
        }

        if (
            !Array.isArray(ids) ||
            ids.length < 1 ||
            ids.length > 500
        ) {
            res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Select between 1 and 500 valid user accounts."
            });

            return null;
        }

        const cleanIds = [...new Set(ids.map(Number))];

        if (
            cleanIds.some(id =>
                !Number.isSafeInteger(id) || id < 1
            )
        ) {
            res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "One or more selected user IDs are invalid."
            });

            return null;
        }

        audienceValue = JSON.stringify(cleanIds);
    }

    if (audienceType === "business_type") {
        if (
            typeof body.audienceValue !== "string" ||
            !body.audienceValue.trim() ||
            body.audienceValue.trim().length > 100
        ) {
            res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Choose a valid business type."
            });

            return null;
        }

        audienceValue = body.audienceValue.trim();
    }

    const isPermanent = parseBoolean(body.isPermanent, false);

    if (isPermanent === null) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid permanent notification setting."
        });

        return null;
    }

    const expiresAt = isPermanent
        ? null
        : validateDate(body.expiresAt, "expiry date", res);

    if (expiresAt === undefined) return null;

    return {
        category: category.database,
        displayCategory: category.display,
        notificationCategory: category.notification,
        title: body.title.trim(),
        message: body.message.trim(),
        audienceType,
        audienceValue,
        isPermanent: isPermanent ? 1 : 0,
        expiresAt
    };
}

// ==========================================
// AUDIENCE QUERY
// ==========================================

function buildAudienceQuery(audienceType, audienceValue) {
    if (audienceType === "all") {
        return {
            sql: `
                SELECT id AS user_id
                FROM users
            `,
            params: []
        };
    }

    if (
        audienceType === "trial" ||
        audienceType === "active" ||
        audienceType === "pending"
    ) {
        return {
            sql: `
                SELECT DISTINCT u.id AS user_id
                FROM users AS u
                INNER JOIN subscriptions AS s
                    ON s.business_id = u.business_id
                WHERE s.status = ?
            `,
            params: [audienceType]
        };
    }

    if (audienceType === "business_type") {
        return {
            sql: `
                SELECT DISTINCT u.id AS user_id
                FROM users AS u
                INNER JOIN businesses AS b
                    ON b.id = u.business_id
                WHERE b.business_type = ?
            `,
            params: [audienceValue]
        };
    }

    if (audienceType === "selected_users") {
        const ids = JSON.parse(audienceValue || "[]");

        if (!ids.length) {
            return {
                sql: "SELECT id AS user_id FROM users WHERE 1 = 0",
                params: []
            };
        }

        return {
            sql: `
                SELECT id AS user_id
                FROM users
                WHERE id IN (${ids.map(() => "?").join(",")})
            `,
            params: ids
        };
    }

    throw new Error("Unsupported notification audience.");
}

async function countAudience(audienceType, audienceValue) {
    const audience = buildAudienceQuery(
        audienceType,
        audienceValue
    );

    const result = await get(
        `
            SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
            FROM (${audience.sql}) AS selected_audience
        `,
        audience.params
    );

    return Number(result?.total || 0);
}

// ==========================================
// AUDIT LOG
// ==========================================

async function recordAudit(
    adminId,
    action,
    targetId,
    details,
    metadata = {}
) {
    await run(
        `INSERT INTO admin_activity_logs (
            admin_user_id,
            action,
            target_type,
            target_id,
            details_json,
            ip_address,
            user_agent
        ) VALUES (?, ?, 'notification', ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            adminId,
            action,
            String(targetId),
            JSON.stringify(details),
            metadata.ipAddress || null,
            metadata.userAgent || null
        ]
    );
}

// ==========================================
// PUBLISH ANNOUNCEMENT
// ==========================================

async function publishAnnouncement(id, adminId, metadata = {}) {
    await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

    try {
        const campaign = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM platform_announcements
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [id]
        );

        if (!campaign) {
            const error = new Error("Notification campaign not found.");
            error.code = "NOT_FOUND";
            throw error;
        }

        if (campaign.status === "published") {
            const error = new Error(
                "This notification has already been published."
            );
            error.code = "CONFLICT";
            throw error;
        }

        if (
            campaign.status !== "draft" &&
            campaign.status !== "scheduled"
        ) {
            const error = new Error(
                "This notification cannot be published in its current status."
            );
            error.code = "CONFLICT";
            throw error;
        }

        const audience = buildAudienceQuery(
            campaign.audience_type,
            campaign.audience_value
        );

        const insertResult = await run(
            `
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO user_notifications (
                    user_id,
                    category,
                    title,
                    message,
                    importance,
                    action_label,
                    action_url,
                    is_permanent,
                    dedupe_key,
                    expires_at
                )
                SELECT
                    audience.user_id,
                    ?,
                    ?,
                    ?,
                    ?,
                    NULL,
                    NULL,
                    ?,
                    'announcement:' || ?,
                    ?
                FROM (${audience.sql}) AS audience
            `,
            [
                campaign.display_category === "welcome"
                    ? "welcome"
                    : campaign.display_category === "announcements"
                        ? "platform_announcement"
                        : campaign.category,
                campaign.title,
                campaign.message,
                campaign.importance || "medium",
                campaign.is_permanent,
                campaign.id,
                campaign.expires_at,
                ...audience.params
            ]
        );

        const updateResult = await run(
            `UPDATE platform_announcements
             SET
                status = 'published',
                published_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                publish_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
             WHERE id = ?
               AND status IN ('draft', 'scheduled')`,
            [id]
        );

        if (updateResult.changes !== 1) {
            const error = new Error(
                "The notification status changed before publishing."
            );
            error.code = "CONFLICT";
            throw error;
        }

        await recordAudit(
            adminId,
            "notification.published",
            id,
            {
                title: campaign.title,
                category: campaign.display_category,
                audienceType: campaign.audience_type,
                recipientsCreated: insertResult.changes
            },
            metadata
        );

        await run("COMMIT");

        return {
            campaign,
            recipientsCreated: insertResult.changes
        };

    } catch (error) {
        try {
            await run("ROLLBACK");
        } catch (rollbackError) {
            console.error(
                "Notification publish rollback failed:",
                rollbackError.message
            );
        }

        throw error;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// SCHEDULED NOTIFICATION PROCESSOR
// ==========================================

let schedulerRunning = false;

async function processDueAnnouncements() {
    if (schedulerRunning) return;

    schedulerRunning = true;

    try {
        const dueAnnouncements = await all(`
            SELECT id, created_by
            FROM platform_announcements
            WHERE status = 'scheduled'
              AND datetime(publish_at) <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            ORDER BY publish_at ASC, id ASC
            LIMIT 50
        `);

        for (const announcement of dueAnnouncements) {
            try {
                await publishAnnouncement(
                    announcement.id,
                    announcement.created_by
                );

                console.log(
                    `Scheduled notification published: ${announcement.id}`
                );

            } catch (error) {
                if (error.code !== "CONFLICT") {
                    console.error(
                        `Scheduled notification ${announcement.id} failed:`,
                        error.message
                    );
                }
            }
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Scheduled notification processor error:",
            error.message
        );

    } finally {
        schedulerRunning = false;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// GET NOTIFICATION CAMPAIGNS
// ==========================================

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view notifications."
            });
        }

        const page = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.page,
            1,
            1000000
        );

        const limit = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.limit,
            10,
            50
        );

        if (
            page === null ||
            limit === null ||
            ![10, 25, 50].includes(limit)
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid pagination parameters."
            });
        }

        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const count = await get(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM platform_announcements"
        );

        const campaigns = await all(
            `SELECT
                id,
                category,
                display_category,
                title,
                message,
                importance,
                audience_type,
                audience_value,
                publish_at,
                expires_at,
                is_permanent,
                status,
                created_by,
                published_at,
                created_at,
                updated_at
             FROM platform_announcements
             ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
             LIMIT ?
             OFFSET ?`,
            [limit, offset]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            campaigns,
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total: Number(count?.total || 0),
                totalPages: Math.ceil(
                    Number(count?.total || 0) / limit
                )
            },
            permissions: {
