                canManage: hasPermission(
                    req,
                    "notifications.manage"
                )
            }
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not retrieve notification campaigns."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// GET NOTIFICATION STATISTICS
// ==========================================

router.get("/stats", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view notification statistics."
            });
        }

        const notificationStats = await get(`
            SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS total,
                SUM(CASE WHEN read_at IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS unread,
                SUM(CASE WHEN read_at IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS read
            FROM user_notifications
        `);

        const campaignStats = await get(`
            SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS total,
                SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'draft' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS drafts,
                SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'scheduled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS scheduled,
                SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'published' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS published
            FROM platform_announcements
        `);

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            notifications: {
                total: Number(notificationStats?.total || 0),
                unread: Number(notificationStats?.unread || 0),
                read: Number(notificationStats?.read || 0)
            },
            campaigns: {
                total: Number(campaignStats?.total || 0),
                drafts: Number(campaignStats?.drafts || 0),
                scheduled: Number(campaignStats?.scheduled || 0),
                published: Number(campaignStats?.published || 0)
            }
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not retrieve notification statistics."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// GET AUDIENCE OPTIONS
// ==========================================

router.get("/audiences", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view notification audiences."
            });
        }

        const businessTypes = await all(`
            SELECT DISTINCT business_type
            FROM businesses
            WHERE business_type IS NOT NULL
              AND TRIM(business_type) != ''
            ORDER BY business_type ASC
        `);

        const audienceCounts = {};

        for (const audienceType of [
            "all",
            "trial",
            "active",
            "pending"
        ]) {
            audienceCounts[audienceType] = await countAudience(
                audienceType,
                null
            );
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            businessTypes: businessTypes.map(
                row => row.business_type
            ),
            counts: audienceCounts
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not retrieve notification audience options."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// PREVIEW NOTIFICATION
// ==========================================

router.post("/preview", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.manage")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to preview notifications."
            });
        }

        const campaign = validateCampaign(req.body || {}, res);
        if (!campaign) return;

        const recipients = await countAudience(
            campaign.audienceType,
            campaign.audienceValue
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            preview: {
                category: campaign.displayCategory,
                title: campaign.title,
                message: campaign.message,
                audienceType: campaign.audienceType,
                recipients,
                isPermanent: campaign.isPermanent === 1,
                expiresAt: campaign.expiresAt
            }
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not preview this notification."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// CREATE DRAFT OR SCHEDULED NOTIFICATION
// ==========================================

router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.manage")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to create notifications."
            });
        }

        const campaign = validateCampaign(req.body || {}, res);
        if (!campaign) return;

        const requestedStatus = req.body.status || "draft";

        if (
            requestedStatus !== "draft" &&
            requestedStatus !== "scheduled"
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "New notifications can only be saved as drafts or scheduled."
            });
        }

        let publishAt = null;

        if (requestedStatus === "scheduled") {
            publishAt = validateDate(
                req.body.publishAt,
                "scheduled publication date",
                res
            );

            if (publishAt === undefined) return;

            if (
                !publishAt ||
                new Date(publishAt).getTime() <= Date.now()
            ) {
                return res.status(400).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Choose a future date and time for scheduling."
                });
            }
        }

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(adminId) || adminId < 1) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Administrator authentication is required."
            });
        }

        const metadata = getRequestMetadata(req);

        await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

        let campaignId;

        try {
            const result = await run(
                `INSERT INTO platform_announcements (
                    category,
                    display_category,
                    title,
                    message,
                    importance,
                    audience_type,
                    audience_value,
                    action_label,
                    action_url,
                    publish_at,
                    expires_at,
                    is_permanent,
                    status,
                    created_by
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'medium', ?, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    campaign.category,
                    campaign.displayCategory,
                    campaign.title,
                    campaign.message,
                    campaign.audienceType,
                    campaign.audienceValue,
                    publishAt || new Date().toISOString(),
                    campaign.expiresAt,
                    campaign.isPermanent,
                    requestedStatus,
                    adminId
                ]
            );

            campaignId = result.lastID;

            await recordAudit(
                adminId,
                requestedStatus === "draft"
                    ? "notification.draft_created"
                    : "notification.scheduled",
                campaignId,
                {
                    title: campaign.title,
                    category: campaign.displayCategory,
                    audienceType: campaign.audienceType,
                    status: requestedStatus
                },
                metadata
            );

            await run("COMMIT");

        } catch (error) {
            await run("ROLLBACK");
            throw error;
        }

        const recipients = await countAudience(
            campaign.audienceType,
            campaign.audienceValue
        );

        return res.status(201).json({
            success: true,
            message: requestedStatus === "draft"
                ? "Notification draft saved."
                : "Notification scheduled successfully.",
            campaign: {
                id: campaignId,
                title: campaign.title,
                category: campaign.displayCategory,
                status: requestedStatus,
                recipients
            }
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not save this notification."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// GET SINGLE CAMPAIGN
// ==========================================

router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view notifications."
            });
        }

        const id = validateId(req.params.id);

        if (!id) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const campaign = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM platform_announcements
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [id]
        );

        if (!campaign) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Notification campaign not found."
            });
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            campaign
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not retrieve this notification."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// EDIT DRAFT
// ==========================================

router.patch("/:id", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.manage")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to edit notifications."
            });
        }

        const id = validateId(req.params.id);

        if (!id) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const campaign = validateCampaign(req.body || {}, res);
        if (!campaign) return;

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);
        const metadata = getRequestMetadata(req);

        await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

        try {
            const result = await run(
                `UPDATE platform_announcements
                 SET
                    category = ?,
                    display_category = ?,
                    title = ?,
                    message = ?,
                    audience_type = ?,
                    audience_value = ?,
                    action_label = NULL,
                    action_url = NULL,
                    expires_at = ?,
                    is_permanent = ?,
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND status = 'draft'`,
                [
                    campaign.category,
                    campaign.displayCategory,
                    campaign.title,
                    campaign.message,
                    campaign.audienceType,
                    campaign.audienceValue,
                    campaign.expiresAt,
                    campaign.isPermanent,
                    id
                ]
            );

            if (result.changes !== 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "Only existing draft notifications can be edited."
                );

                error.code = "CONFLICT";
                throw error;
            }

            await recordAudit(
                adminId,
                "notification.draft_updated",
                id,
                {
                    title: campaign.title,
                    category: campaign.displayCategory,
                    audienceType: campaign.audienceType
                },
                metadata
            );

            await run("COMMIT");

        } catch (error) {
            await run("ROLLBACK");
            throw error;
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Notification draft updated."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        if (error.code === "CONFLICT") {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not update this notification draft."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// PUBLISH NOW
// ==========================================

router.post("/:id/publish", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.manage")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to publish notifications."
            });
        }

        const id = validateId(req.params.id);

        if (!id) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

        const result = await publishAnnouncement(
            id,
            adminId,
            getRequestMetadata(req)
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Notification published successfully.",
            recipientsCreated: result.recipientsCreated
        });

    } catch (error) {
        if (error.code === "NOT_FOUND") {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        if (error.code === "CONFLICT") {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not publish this notification."
        );
    }
});

// ==========================================
// CANCEL SCHEDULED NOTIFICATION
// ==========================================

router.post("/:id/cancel", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "notifications.manage")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to cancel notifications."
            });
        }

        const id = validateId(req.params.id);

        if (!id) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);
        const metadata = getRequestMetadata(req);

        await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");

        try {
            const result = await run(
                `UPDATE platform_announcements
                 SET
                    status = 'cancelled',
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND status = 'scheduled'`,
                [id]
            );

            if (result.changes !== 1) {
                const error = new Error(
                    "Only scheduled notifications can be cancelled."
                );

                error.code = "CONFLICT";
                throw error;
            }

            await recordAudit(
                adminId,
                "notification.cancelled",
                id,
                {
                    reason: "Scheduled notification cancelled by administrator."
                },
                metadata
            );

            await run("COMMIT");

        } catch (error) {
            await run("ROLLBACK");
            throw error;
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Scheduled notification cancelled."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        if (error.code === "CONFLICT") {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                message: error.message
            });
        }

        return sendError(
            res,
            error,
            "Could not cancel this scheduled notification."
        );
    }
});

// Exposed for the server's background scheduler.
router.processDueAnnouncements = processDueAnnouncements;

module.exports = router;

========== adminOverviewRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAdminAuth);

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

// Check permission to view platform statistics.
function requireDashboardPermission(req, res, next) {
    if (
        !req.adminPermissions ||
        !req.adminPermissions.has("dashboard.view")
    ) {
        return res.status(403).json({
            success: false,
            message: "You do not have permission to view the Admin Overview."
        });
    }

    next();
}

// GET ADMIN OVERVIEW STATISTICS
router.get("/", requireDashboardPermission, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const stats = await get(`
            SELECT
                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*)
                    FROM businesses
                ) AS total_customers,

                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*)
                    FROM subscriptions
                    WHERE status = 'active'
                ) AS active_subscriptions,

                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*)
                    FROM subscriptions
                    WHERE status = 'trial'
                ) AS trial_customers,

                (
                    SELECT COUNT(*)
                    FROM subscription_payments
                    WHERE status = 'pending'
                ) AS pending_payments
        `);

        if (!stats) {
            throw new Error("Platform statistics could not be retrieved.");
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            stats: {
                totalCustomers: Number(stats.total_customers) || 0,
                activeSubscriptions: Number(stats.active_subscriptions) || 0,
                trialCustomers: Number(stats.trial_customers) || 0,
                pendingPayments: Number(stats.pending_payments) || 0
            },
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Admin Overview statistics error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load platform statistics."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== adminSubscriptionsRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAdminAuth = require("../middleware/requireAdminAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAdminAuth);

// ==========================================
// DATABASE HELPERS
// ==========================================

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function parsePositiveInteger(value, fallback, maximum) {
    if (value === undefined) return fallback;

    const parsed = Number(value);

    if (
        !Number.isSafeInteger(parsed) ||
        parsed < 1 ||
        parsed > maximum
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return parsed;
}

function escapeLike(value) {
    return value.replace(/[\\%_]/g, "\\$&");
}

function sqlLiteral(value) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
        return "NULL";
    }

    return `'${String(value).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
}

function hasPermission(req, permission) {
    return req.adminPermissions instanceof Set &&
        req.adminPermissions.has(permission);
}

function validateBusinessId(value) {
    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(value)
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const id = Number(value);

    return Number.isSafeInteger(id) ? id : null;
}

function validateReason(req, res) {
    const reason = req.body?.reason;

    if (typeof reason !== "string") {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "An action reason is required."
        });

        return null;
    }

    const cleanReason = reason.trim();

    if (cleanReason.length < 5 || cleanReason.length > 500) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The reason must contain between 5 and 500 characters."
        });

        return null;
    }

    return cleanReason;
}

function validateBillingCycle(value) {
    return value === "monthly" || value === "annual"
        ? value
        : null;
}

function validateActivationType(value) {
    if (value === undefined) return "manual";

    return value === "manual" || value === "complimentary"
        ? value
        : null;
}

function getPlanPrice(cycle) {
    return cycle === "monthly" ? 1000 : 10000;
}

// ==========================================
// DATE HELPERS
// ==========================================

function getUtcDate() {
    return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function getUtcDateTime() {
    return new Date().toISOString()
        .replace("T", " ")
        .slice(0, 19);
}

function validateStartDate(value) {
    if (value === undefined) {
        return getUtcDate();
    }

    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`);

    if (
        Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ||
        date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== value
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return value;
}

function normalizeDateTime(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    const normalized = String(value).replace(" ", "T");

    const date = new Date(
        normalized.endsWith("Z")
            ? normalized
            : `${normalized}Z`
    );

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return null;
    }

    return date.toISOString()
        .replace("T", " ")
        .slice(0, 19);
}

function isFutureDate(value) {
    if (!value) return false;

    const normalized = normalizeDateTime(value);

    if (!normalized) return false;

    return new Date(`${normalized.replace(" ", "T")}Z`).getTime()
        > Date.now();
}

// Adds calendar months while preserving the time of day.
// The day is clamped to the last valid day of the destination month.
function addMonthsToDateTime(value, months) {
    const normalized = normalizeDateTime(value);

    if (!normalized) {
        throw new Error("Invalid subscription start date.");
    }

    const date = new Date(`${normalized.replace(" ", "T")}Z`);

    const originalDay = date.getUTCDate();

    date.setUTCDate(1);
    date.setUTCMonth(date.getUTCMonth() + months);

    const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(
        date.getUTCFullYear(),
        date.getUTCMonth() + 1,
        0
    )).getUTCDate();

    date.setUTCDate(Math.min(originalDay, lastDay));

    return date.toISOString()
        .replace("T", " ")
        .slice(0, 19);
}

function dateToDateTime(date) {
    return `${date} 00:00:00`;
}

function getCurrentStatus(subscription) {
    if (!subscription) return "not_started";

    if (
        subscription.status === "trial" &&
        subscription.trial_ends_at &&
        !isFutureDate(subscription.trial_ends_at)
    ) {
        return "expired";
    }

    if (
        subscription.status === "active" &&
        subscription.current_period_end &&
        !isFutureDate(subscription.current_period_end)
    ) {
        return "expired";
    }

    return subscription.status;
}

// ==========================================
// TRANSACTION HELPER
// ==========================================

function executeTransaction(sql) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.exec(sql, error => {
            if (!error) {
                return resolve();
            }

            db.exec("ROLLBACK", rollbackError => {
                if (rollbackError) {
                    console.error(
                        "Subscription transaction rollback failed:",
                        rollbackError.message
                    );
                }

                reject(error);
            });
        });
    });
}

// ==========================================
// AUDIT LOG HELPER
// ==========================================

function buildAuditSql(req, settings) {
    const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

    if (
        !Number.isSafeInteger(adminId) ||
        adminId < 1
    ) {
        throw new Error("Invalid administrator identity.");
    }

    const details = JSON.stringify(settings.details);

    const ipAddress = req.ip || null;

    const userAgent = req.get("user-agent")
        ? req.get("user-agent").slice(0, 500)
        : null;

    return `
        INSERT INTO admin_activity_logs (
            admin_user_id,
            action,
            target_type,
            target_id,
            details_json,
            ip_address,
            user_agent
        )
        SELECT
            ${adminId},
            ${sqlLiteral(settings.action)},
            'subscription',
            ${sqlLiteral(String(settings.businessId))},
            ${sqlLiteral(details)},
            ${sqlLiteral(ipAddress)},
            ${sqlLiteral(userAgent)}
        WHERE changes() = 1;
    `;
}

// ==========================================
// SUBSCRIPTION PRICE AND PLAN INFORMATION
// ==========================================

function buildPlanInformation(cycle, activationType) {
    const standardPrice = getPlanPrice(cycle);

    return {
        planName: "WIN Pro",
        billingCycle: cycle,
        standardPrice,
        currency: "KES",
        activationType,
        complimentary: activationType === "complimentary"
    };
}

// ==========================================
// GET SUBSCRIPTION LIST
// ==========================================

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "subscriptions.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view subscriptions."
            });
        }

        const page = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.page,
            1,
            1000000
        );

        const limit = parsePositiveInteger(
            req.query.limit,
            10,
            50
        );

        if (
            page === null ||
            limit === null ||
            ![10, 25, 50].includes(limit)
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid pagination parameters."
            });
        }

        const rawSearch = req.query.search ?? "";

        if (typeof rawSearch !== "string") {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid search value."
            });
        }

        const search = rawSearch.trim();

        if (search.length > 100) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Search must not exceed 100 characters."
            });
        }

        const searchSql = search
            ? `
                WHERE (
                    businesses.business_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                    OR businesses.business_type LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                    OR owner.full_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                    OR owner.email LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                    OR businesses.email LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'
                )
            `
            : "";

        const searchPattern = `%${escapeLike(search)}%`;

        const searchParams = search
            ? Array(5).fill(searchPattern)
            : [];

        const joins = `
            LEFT JOIN users AS owner
                ON owner.id = (
                    SELECT account_owner.id
                    FROM users AS account_owner
                    WHERE account_owner.business_id = businesses.id
                    ORDER BY
                        CASE
                            WHEN account_owner.role = 'owner' THEN 0
                            ELSE 1
                        END,
                        account_owner.id ASC
                    LIMIT 1
                )

            LEFT JOIN subscriptions AS subscription
                ON subscription.business_id = businesses.id
        `;

        const count = await get(
            `
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
                FROM businesses
                ${joins}
                ${searchSql}
            `,
            searchParams
        );

        const total = Number(count?.total || 0);
        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const subscriptions = await all(
            `
                SELECT
                    businesses.id AS business_id,
                    businesses.business_name,
                    businesses.business_type,

                    owner.full_name AS owner_name,
                    owner.email AS owner_email,

                    subscription.id AS subscription_id,
                    subscription.plan_name,
                    subscription.status,
                    subscription.billing_cycle,
                    subscription.activation_type,
                    subscription.trial_started_at,
                    subscription.trial_ends_at,
                    subscription.current_period_start,
                    subscription.current_period_end,
                    subscription.updated_at

                FROM businesses

                ${joins}

                ${searchSql}

                ORDER BY businesses.created_at DESC,
                         businesses.id DESC

                LIMIT ?
                OFFSET ?
            `,
            [
                ...searchParams,
                limit,
                offset
            ]
        );

        const formatted = subscriptions.map(item => ({
            ...item,
            current_status: getCurrentStatus(
                item.subscription_id
                    ? item
                    : null
            )
        }));

        const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit);

        return res.json({
            success: true,

            subscriptions: formatted,

            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages,
                hasNext: page < totalPages,
                hasPrevious: page > 1
            },

            search,

            permissions: {
                canView: true,
                canActivate: hasPermission(
                    req,
                    "subscriptions.activate"
                ),
                canRenew: hasPermission(
                    req,
                    "subscriptions.renew"
                )
            },

            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Admin Subscriptions API error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not retrieve subscriptions."
        });
    }
});

// ==========================================
// GET SUBSCRIPTION HISTORY
// ==========================================

router.get("/:businessId/history", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "subscriptions.view")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to view subscription history."
            });
        }

        const businessId = validateBusinessId(
            req.params.businessId
        );

        if (!businessId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid business account ID."
            });
        }

        const business = await get(
            `SELECT id, business_name
             FROM businesses
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!business) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Business account not found."
            });
        }

        const history = await all(
            `
                SELECT
                    logs.id,
                    logs.action,
                    logs.details_json,
                    logs.created_at,
                    admin.full_name AS administrator_name

                FROM admin_activity_logs AS logs

                LEFT JOIN admin_users AS admin
                    ON admin.id = logs.admin_user_id

                WHERE logs.target_type = 'subscription'
                  AND logs.target_id = ?

                ORDER BY logs.created_at DESC, logs.id DESC

                LIMIT 50
            `,
            [String(businessId)]
        );

        const formattedHistory = history.map(item => {
            let details = {};

            try {
                details = JSON.parse(item.details_json || "{}");
            } catch (error) {
                details = {
                    message: "History details could not be decoded."
                };
            }

            return {
                id: item.id,
                action: item.action,
                details,
                administratorName:
                    item.administrator_name || "Former administrator",
                createdAt: item.created_at
            };
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            business,
            history: formattedHistory
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Subscription history error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not retrieve subscription history."
        });
    }
});

// ==========================================
// MANUALLY ACTIVATE SUBSCRIPTION
// ==========================================

router.post("/:businessId/activate", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "subscriptions.activate")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to activate subscriptions."
            });
        }

        const businessId = validateBusinessId(
            req.params.businessId
        );

        if (!businessId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid business account ID."
            });
        }

        const cycle = validateBillingCycle(
            req.body?.billingCycle
        );

        if (!cycle) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Choose monthly or annual billing."
            });
        }

        const activationType = validateActivationType(
            req.body?.activationType
        );

        if (!activationType) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid activation type."
            });
        }

        const reason = validateReason(req, res);

        if (!reason) return;

        const startDate = validateStartDate(
            req.body?.startDate
        );

        if (!startDate) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Enter a valid activation date in YYYY-MM-DD format."
            });
        }

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(adminId) || adminId < 1) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Administrator authentication is required."
            });
        }

        const business = await get(
            `SELECT id, business_name
             FROM businesses
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!business) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Business account not found."
            });
        }

        const existing = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM subscriptions
             WHERE business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (
            existing &&
            existing.status === "active" &&
            isFutureDate(existing.current_period_end)
        ) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "SUBSCRIPTION_ALREADY_ACTIVE",
                message: "This subscription is already active. Use renewal to extend it."
            });
        }

        const startDateTime = dateToDateTime(startDate);

        const months = cycle === "monthly" ? 1 : 12;

        const endDateTime = addMonthsToDateTime(
            startDateTime,
            months
        );

        const plan = buildPlanInformation(
            cycle,
            activationType
        );

        const details = {
            businessId,
            businessName: business.business_name,
            action: "manual_activation",
            planName: "WIN Pro",
            billingCycle: cycle,
            standardPrice: plan.standardPrice,
            currency: "KES",
            activationType,
            startDate: startDateTime,
            endDate: endDateTime,
            previousStatus: existing?.status || "not_started",
            reason
        };

        const auditSql = buildAuditSql(req, {
            action: "subscription.manually_activated",
            businessId,
            details
        });

        await executeTransaction(`
            BEGIN IMMEDIATE;

            INSERT INTO subscriptions (
                business_id,
                plan_name,
                status,
                trial_started_at,
                trial_ends_at,
                current_period_start,
                current_period_end,
                billing_cycle,
                activation_type,
                created_at,
                updated_at
            )
            VALUES (
                ${businessId},
                'WIN Pro',
                'active',
                NULL,
                NULL,
                ${sqlLiteral(startDateTime)},
                ${sqlLiteral(endDateTime)},
                ${sqlLiteral(cycle)},
                ${sqlLiteral(activationType)},
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
            ON CONFLICT(business_id) DO UPDATE SET
                plan_name = 'WIN Pro',
                status = 'active',
                trial_ends_at = NULL,
                current_period_start = excluded.current_period_start,
                current_period_end = excluded.current_period_end,
                billing_cycle = excluded.billing_cycle,
                activation_type = excluded.activation_type,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE
                subscriptions.status != 'active'
                OR subscriptions.current_period_end IS NULL
                OR datetime(subscriptions.current_period_end) <= datetime('now');

            ${auditSql}

            COMMIT;
        `);

        const updated = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM subscriptions
             WHERE business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (
            !updated ||
            updated.current_period_start !== startDateTime ||
            updated.current_period_end !== endDateTime ||
            updated.billing_cycle !== cycle
        ) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "SUBSCRIPTION_ACTIVATION_CONFLICT",
                message: "The subscription changed during activation. Refresh and try again."
            });
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: activationType === "complimentary"
                ? "Complimentary subscription activated successfully."
                : "Subscription activated successfully.",
            subscription: updated
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Manual subscription activation failed:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not activate the subscription."
        });
    }
});

// ==========================================
// RENEW OR EXTEND SUBSCRIPTION
// ==========================================

router.post("/:businessId/renew", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        if (!hasPermission(req, "subscriptions.renew")) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "You do not have permission to renew subscriptions."
            });
        }

        const businessId = validateBusinessId(
            req.params.businessId
        );

        if (!businessId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid business account ID."
            });
        }

        const cycle = validateBillingCycle(
            req.body?.billingCycle
        );

        if (!cycle) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Choose monthly or annual billing."
            });
        }

        const activationType = validateActivationType(
            req.body?.activationType
        );

        if (!activationType) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid activation type."
            });
        }

        const reason = validateReason(req, res);

        if (!reason) return;

        const adminId = Number(req.adminUser?.id);

        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(adminId) || adminId < 1) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Administrator authentication is required."
            });
        }

        const business = await get(
            `SELECT id, business_name
             FROM businesses
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!business) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Business account not found."
            });
        }

        const existing = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM subscriptions
             WHERE business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (!existing) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_FOUND",
                message: "Activate a subscription before renewing it."
            });
        }

        // Continue from the remaining paid period.
        // If a trial is still running, begin the paid period after the trial.
        // Otherwise, begin from the current UTC date and time.
        let startDateTime;

        if (isFutureDate(existing.current_period_end)) {
            startDateTime = normalizeDateTime(
                existing.current_period_end
            );
        } else if (isFutureDate(existing.trial_ends_at)) {
            startDateTime = normalizeDateTime(
                existing.trial_ends_at
            );
        } else {
            startDateTime = getUtcDateTime();
        }

        const months = cycle === "monthly" ? 1 : 12;

        const endDateTime = addMonthsToDateTime(
            startDateTime,
            months
        );

        const plan = buildPlanInformation(
            cycle,
            activationType
        );

        const details = {
            businessId,
            businessName: business.business_name,
            action: "subscription_renewal",
            planName: "WIN Pro",
            billingCycle: cycle,
            standardPrice: plan.standardPrice,
            currency: "KES",
            activationType,
            previousStatus: existing.status,
            previousPeriodEnd: existing.current_period_end,
            startDate: startDateTime,
            endDate: endDateTime,
            reason
        };

        const auditSql = buildAuditSql(req, {
            action: "subscription.renewed",
            businessId,
            details
        });

        const previousEnd = existing.current_period_end || "";
        const previousStatus = existing.status;

        await executeTransaction(`
            BEGIN IMMEDIATE;

            UPDATE subscriptions
            SET
                plan_name = 'WIN Pro',
                status = 'active',
                trial_ends_at = NULL,
                current_period_start = ${sqlLiteral(startDateTime)},
                current_period_end = ${sqlLiteral(endDateTime)},
                billing_cycle = ${sqlLiteral(cycle)},
                activation_type = ${sqlLiteral(activationType)},
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE
                business_id = ${businessId}
                AND status = ${sqlLiteral(previousStatus)}
                AND COALESCE(current_period_end, '') = ${sqlLiteral(previousEnd)};

            ${auditSql}

            COMMIT;
        `);

        const updated = await get(
            `SELECT *
             FROM subscriptions
             WHERE business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        );

        if (
            !updated ||
            updated.current_period_start !== startDateTime ||
            updated.current_period_end !== endDateTime ||
            updated.billing_cycle !== cycle
        ) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                success: false,
                code: "SUBSCRIPTION_RENEWAL_CONFLICT",
                message: "The subscription changed during renewal. Refresh and try again."
            });
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: activationType === "complimentary"
                ? "Complimentary subscription extension completed."
                : "Subscription renewed successfully.",
            subscription: updated
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Subscription renewal failed:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not renew the subscription."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== authRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");
const db = require("../config/database");

const {
    sendVerificationEmail
} = require("../services/emailVerificationService");

const {
    sendLoginConfirmationEmail
} = require("../services/loginConfirmationService");

const {
    consumeEmailToken
} = require("../services/emailTokenService");

const {
    sendEmail
} = require("../services/emailService");

const router = express.Router();

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function recordSecurityEvent(userId, email, eventType, req) {
    db.run(
        `INSERT INTO email_security_events (
            user_id,
            email,
            event_type,
            ip_address,
            user_agent
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            userId || null,
            email || null,
            eventType,
            req.ip || null,
            req.get("user-agent") || null
        ],
        error => {
            if (error) {
                console.error(
                    "Security event logging failed:",
                    error.message
                );
            }
        }
    );
}

async function logEmailDelivery(
    userId,
    email,
    type,
    subject,
    status,
    messageId = null,
    errorMessage = null
) {
    await run(
        `INSERT INTO email_delivery_logs (
            user_id,
            recipient_email,
            email_type,
            subject,
            status,
            provider_message_id,
            error_message
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            userId,
            email,
            type,
            subject,
            status,
            messageId,
            errorMessage
                ? String(errorMessage).slice(0, 500)
                : null
        ]
    );
}

function escapeHtml(value) {
    const replacements = {
        "&": "&amp;",
        "<": "&lt;",
        ">": "&gt;",
        '"': "&quot;",
        "'": "&#39;"
    };

    return String(value || "").replace(
        /[&<>"']/g,
        character => replacements[character]
    );
}

async function sendWelcomeEmail(user) {
    const subject = "Welcome to WIN Business Suite";

    const safeName = escapeHtml(user.full_name);

    const text = [
        `Hello ${user.full_name},`,
        "",
        "Your email address has been successfully verified.",
        "Welcome to WIN Business Suite.",
        "",
        "You can now log in and continue setting up your business.",
        "",
        "WIN Business Suite Team",
        "Manage Everything. Grow Smarter."
    ].join("\n");

    const html = `
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="en">
        <head>
            <meta charset="UTF-8">
            <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
            <title>Welcome to WIN Business Suite</title>
        </head>
        <body style="margin:0;padding:30px 12px;background:#f3f6fb;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#17233c;">
            <div style="max-width:560px;margin:0 auto;background:#ffffff;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid #e4eaf2;">
                <div style="background:#101d3a;padding:30px;text-align:center;">
                    <h1 style="margin:0;color:#ffffff;font-size:27px;">WIN <span style="color:#25c4e8;">Business Suite</span></h1>
                    <p style="margin:10px 0 0;color:#d8e5f7;font-size:13px;">Manage Everything. Grow Smarter.</p>
                </div>
                <div style="padding:32px 28px;">
                    <h2 style="color:#101d3a;">Welcome aboard!</h2>
                    <p>Hello ${safeName},</p>
                    <p>Your email address has been successfully verified. Welcome to WIN Business Suite!</p>
                    <p>You can now log in and continue setting up your business.</p>
                </div>
                <div style="background:#f3f6fb;padding:20px;text-align:center;color:#68758a;font-size:12px;">
                    WIN Business Suite Team<br>
                    Bori Technologies Group
                </div>
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
    `;

    const result = await sendEmail({
        to: user.email,
        subject,
        text,
        html
    });

    await logEmailDelivery(
        user.id,
        user.email,
        "welcome",
        subject,
        "sent",
        result.messageId
    );
}

// REGISTER
router.post("/register", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const {
            business_name,
            business_type,
            full_name,
            email,
            password
        } = req.body;

        if (
            typeof business_name !== "string" ||
            typeof business_type !== "string" ||
            typeof full_name !== "string" ||
            typeof email !== "string" ||
            typeof password !== "string"
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "All fields are required."
            });
        }

        const cleanBusinessName = business_name.trim();
        const cleanBusinessType = business_type.trim();
        const cleanFullName = full_name.trim();
        const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

        if (
            !cleanBusinessName ||
            !cleanBusinessType ||
            !cleanFullName ||
            !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(normalizedEmail)
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Please provide valid registration details."
            });
        }

        if (password.length < 12 || password.length > 128) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Password must contain 12 to 128 characters."
            });
        }

        const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);

        db.serialize(() => {
            db.run("BEGIN TRANSACTION", beginError => {
                if (beginError) {
                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Could not begin registration."
                    });
                }

                function rollbackWithResponse(message, statusCode) {
                    db.run("ROLLBACK", rollbackError => {
                        if (rollbackError) {
                            console.error(
                                "Registration rollback failed:",
                                rollbackError.message
                            );
                        }

                        if (!res.headersSent) {
                            res.status(statusCode).json({
                                success: false,
                                message
                            });
                        }
                    });
                }

                db.run(
                    `INSERT INTO businesses (
                        business_name,
                        business_type
                    ) VALUES (?, ?)`,
                    [cleanBusinessName, cleanBusinessType],
                    function (businessError) {
                        if (businessError) {
                            return rollbackWithResponse(
                                "Could not create business.",
                                500
                            );
                        }

                        const businessId = this.lastID;

                        db.run(
                            `INSERT INTO users (
                                business_id,
                                full_name,
                                email,
                                password_hash,
                                role,
                                email_verified
                            )
                            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'owner', 0)`,
                            [
                                businessId,
                                cleanFullName,
                                normalizedEmail,
                                passwordHash
                            ],
                            function (userError) {
                                if (userError) {
                                    return rollbackWithResponse(
                                        "Registration failed. This email may already be registered.",
                                        409
                                    );
                                }

                                const userId = this.lastID;

                                db.run(
                                    `INSERT INTO subscriptions (
                                        business_id,
                                        plan_name,
                                        status,
                                        trial_started_at,
                                        trial_ends_at
                                    )
                                    VALUES (?, 'WIN Pro', 'pending', NULL, NULL)`,
                                    [businessId],
                                    subscriptionError => {
                                        if (subscriptionError) {
                                            console.error(
                                                "Subscription creation error:",
                                                subscriptionError.message
                                            );

                                            return rollbackWithResponse(
                                                "Could not complete subscription setup.",
                                                500
                                            );
                                        }

                                        db.run("COMMIT", async commitError => {
                                            if (commitError) {
                                                return db.run(
                                                    "ROLLBACK",
                                                    () => {
                                                        res.status(500).json({
                                                            success: false,
                                                            message: "Could not complete registration."
                                                        });
                                                    }
                                                );
                                            }

                                            recordSecurityEvent(
                                                userId,
                                                normalizedEmail,
                                                "account_registered",
                                                req
                                            );

                                            try {
                                                await sendVerificationEmail({
                                                    id: userId,
                                                    full_name: cleanFullName,
                                                    email: normalizedEmail
                                                });

                                                return res.status(201).json({
                                                    success: true,
                                                    emailSent: true,
                                                    message: "Your account has been created. We have sent a verification link to your email address. The link expires in 10 minutes. Please verify your email before logging in.",
                                                    business_id: businessId,
                                                    subscription: {
                                                        plan: "WIN Pro",
                                                        status: "pending",
                                                        trial_started: false,
                                                        duration_days: 7
                                                    }
                                                });
                                            } catch (emailError) {
                                                console.error(
                                                    "Verification email failed:",
                                                    emailError.message
                                                );

                                                return res.status(201).json({
                                                    success: true,
                                                    emailSent: false,
                                                    message: "Your account has been created, but we could not send the verification email. Please use the resend verification option.",
                                                    business_id: businessId,
                                                    subscription: {
                                                        plan: "WIN Pro",
                                                        status: "pending",
                                                        trial_started: false,
                                                        duration_days: 7
                                                    }
                                                });
                                            }
                                        });
                                    }
                                );
                            }
                        );
                    }
                );
            });
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Registration error:", error.message);

        if (!res.headersSent) {
            res.status(500).json({
                success: false,
                message: "An unexpected error occurred."
            });
        }
    }
});

// VERIFY EMAIL
router.post("/verify-email", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { token } = req.body;

        if (typeof token !== "string" || !token) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "A verification token is required."
            });
        }

        const userId = await consumeEmailToken(
            token,
            "email_verification"
        );

        if (!userId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This verification link is invalid, expired, or has already been used."
            });
        }

        const updateResult = await run(
            `UPDATE users
             SET email_verified = 1,
                 email_verified_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
             WHERE id = ?
             AND email_verified = 0`,
            [userId]
        );

        if (updateResult.changes !== 1) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This account is already verified or is no longer available."
            });
        }

        const user = await get(
            `SELECT id, full_name, email
             FROM users
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [userId]
        );

        if (!user) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Account not found."
            });
        }

        recordSecurityEvent(
            user.id,
            user.email,
            "email_verified",
            req
        );

        try {
            await sendWelcomeEmail(user);
        } catch (welcomeError) {
            console.error(
                "Welcome email failed:",
                welcomeError.message
            );

            try {
                await logEmailDelivery(
                    user.id,
                    user.email,
                    "welcome",
                    "Welcome to WIN Business Suite",
                    "failed",
                    null,
                    welcomeError.message
                );
            } catch (logError) {
                console.error(
                    "Welcome email log failed:",
                    logError.message
                );
            }
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Your email has been verified successfully. You can now log in to WIN Business Suite."
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Email verification error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Email verification could not be completed. Please try again."
        });
    }
});

// RESEND VERIFICATION EMAIL
router.post("/resend-verification", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const email = req.body.email;

        if (typeof email !== "string" || !email.trim()) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Please enter your email address."
            });
        }

        const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

        const user = await get(
            `SELECT id, full_name, email, email_verified
             FROM users
             WHERE email = ?`,
            [normalizedEmail]
        );

        const genericMessage =
            "If an unverified account exists for this email, a new verification link will be sent.";

        if (!user || Number(user.email_verified) === 1) {
            return res.json({
                success: true,
                message: genericMessage
            });
        }

        try {
            await sendVerificationEmail(user);

            recordSecurityEvent(
                user.id,
                user.email,
                "verification_email_resent",
                req
            );
        } catch (emailError) {
            console.error(
                "Resend verification failed:",
                emailError.message
            );
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: genericMessage
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Resend verification error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not process your request. Please try again."
        });
    }
});

// LOGIN
// Password verification does not create a session.
// A fresh email confirmation link is required for every login.
router.post("/login", (req, res) => {
    const { email, password } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof email !== "string" ||
        typeof password !== "string" ||
        !email.trim() ||
        !password
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Email and password are required."
        });
    }

    const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            id,
            business_id,
            full_name,
            email,
            password_hash,
            role,
            email_verified
         FROM users
         WHERE email = ?`,
        [normalizedEmail],
        async (error, user) => {
            if (error) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Login could not be completed."
                });
            }

            if (!user) {
                recordSecurityEvent(
                    null,
                    normalizedEmail,
                    "login_failed",
                    req
                );

                return res.status(401).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid email or password."
                });
            }

            try {
                const passwordMatches = await bcrypt.compare(
                    password,
                    user.password_hash
                );

                if (!passwordMatches) {
                    recordSecurityEvent(
                        user.id,
                        user.email,
                        "login_failed",
                        req
                    );

                    return res.status(401).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Invalid email or password."
                    });
                }

                if (Number(user.email_verified) !== 1) {
                    recordSecurityEvent(
                        user.id,
                        user.email,
                        "login_blocked_unverified",
                        req
                    );

                    return res.status(403).json({
                        success: false,
                        emailVerificationRequired: true,
                        message: "Please verify your email address before logging in."
                    });
                }

                // Remove any existing session in this browser.
                // This password check must never leave an authenticated session.
                const sendConfirmation = async () => {
                    try {
                        await sendLoginConfirmationEmail({
                            id: user.id,
                            full_name: user.full_name,
                            email: user.email
                        });

                        recordSecurityEvent(
                            user.id,
                            user.email,
                            "login_confirmation_requested",
                            req
                        );

                        return res.json({
                            success: true,
                            confirmationRequired: true,
                            message: "A fresh login confirmation link has been sent to your email. It expires in 10 minutes. Open it and press Confirm Login to continue."
                        });
                    } catch (emailError) {
                        console.error(
                            "Login confirmation email failed:",
                            emailError.message
                        );

                        recordSecurityEvent(
                            user.id,
                            user.email,
                            "login_confirmation_email_failed",
                            req
                        );

                        return res.status(503).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "We could not send your login confirmation email. Please try again."
                        });
                    }
                };

                if (
                    req.session &&
                    typeof req.session.destroy === "function"
                ) {
                    return req.session.destroy(sessionError => {
                        if (sessionError) {
                            console.error(
                                "Previous session cleanup failed:",
                                sessionError.message
                            );

                            return res.status(500).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "Could not prepare a secure login. Please try again."
                            });
                        }

                        res.clearCookie("win.sid", {
                            httpOnly: true,
                            secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
                            sameSite: "lax"
                        });

                        sendConfirmation();
                    });
                }

                return sendConfirmation();
            } catch (compareError) {
                console.error(
                    "Login error:",
                    compareError.message
                );

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Login could not be completed."
                });
            }
        }
    );
});

// CONFIRM LOGIN
// The session is created only after the user opens the email link
// and explicitly presses the Confirm Login button.
router.post("/confirm-login", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { token } = req.body;

        if (typeof token !== "string" || !token) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "A login confirmation token is required."
            });
        }

        const userId = await consumeEmailToken(
            token,
            "login_confirmation"
        );

        if (!userId) {
            recordSecurityEvent(
                null,
                null,
                "login_confirmation_invalid",
                req
            );

            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This login confirmation link is invalid, expired, or has already been used. Please log in again."
            });
        }

        const user = await get(
            `SELECT
                id,
                business_id,
                full_name,
                email,
                role,
                email_verified
             FROM users
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [userId]
        );

        if (!user || Number(user.email_verified) !== 1) {
            return res.status(403).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This account is not verified or is no longer available."
            });
        }

        req.session.regenerate(sessionError => {
            if (sessionError) {
                console.error(
                    "Login session regeneration failed:",
                    sessionError.message
                );

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not start your secure session. Please log in again."
                });
            }

            req.session.user = {
                id: user.id,
                business_id: user.business_id,
                full_name: user.full_name,
                email: user.email,
                role: user.role
            };

            req.session.save(saveError => {
                if (saveError) {
                    console.error(
                        "Login session save failed:",
                        saveError.message
                    );

                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Could not save your secure session. Please log in again."
                    });
                }

                recordSecurityEvent(
                    user.id,
                    user.email,
                    "login_confirmed",
                    req
                );

                return res.json({
                    success: true,
                    message: "Your login has been confirmed successfully."
                });
            });
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Login confirmation error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Login confirmation could not be completed. Please try again."
        });
    }
});

// CHECK CURRENT SESSION AND BUSINESS NAME
router.get("/me", (req, res) => {
    if (!req.session || !req.session.user) {
        return res.status(401).json({
            success: false,
            message: "You are not logged in."
        });
    }

    const userId = req.session.user.id;
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            users.id,
            users.business_id,
            users.full_name,
            users.email,
            users.role,
            users.email_verified,
            businesses.business_name
         FROM users
