         INNER JOIN businesses
            ON users.business_id = businesses.id
         WHERE users.id = ?
         AND users.business_id = ?`,
        [userId, businessId],
        (error, user) => {
            if (error) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load account information."
                });
            }

            if (!user) {
                return res.status(401).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Account not found."
                });
            }

            if (Number(user.email_verified) !== 1) {
                return res.status(403).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Please verify your email address."
                });
            }

            return res.json({
                success: true,
                user
            });
        }
    );
});

// LOGOUT
router.post("/logout", (req, res) => {
    if (!req.session) {
        res.clearCookie("win.sid");

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Logged out successfully."
        });
    }

    req.session.destroy(error => {
        if (error) {
            return res.status(500).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Could not log out."
            });
        }

        res.clearCookie("win.sid", {
            httpOnly: true,
            secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
            sameSite: "lax"
        });

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Logged out successfully."
        });
    });
});

module.exports = router;

========== businessSettingsRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const router = express.Router();

const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

// GET BUSINESS SETTINGS
router.get("/", requireAuth, (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            id,
            business_name,
            business_type,
            email,
            phone,
            country,
            currency,
            address,
            registration_number
         FROM businesses
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [businessId],
        (error, business) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Business settings load error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load business settings."
                });
            }

            if (!business) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Business not found."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                business
            });
        }
    );
});

// UPDATE BUSINESS SETTINGS
router.put("/", requireAuth, (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    const {
        business_name,
        business_type,
        email,
        phone,
        country,
        currency,
        address,
        registration_number
    } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof business_name !== "string" ||
        !business_name.trim() ||
        typeof business_type !== "string" ||
        !business_type.trim()
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Business name and business type are required."
        });
    }

    const cleanName = business_name.trim();
    const cleanType = business_type.trim();
    const cleanEmail = typeof email === "string" ? email.trim() : "";
    const cleanPhone = typeof phone === "string" ? phone.trim() : "";
    const cleanCountry = typeof country === "string" ? country.trim() : "Kenya";
    const cleanCurrency = typeof currency === "string"
        ? currency.trim().toUpperCase()
        : "KES";
    const cleanAddress = typeof address === "string" ? address.trim() : "";
    const cleanRegistration = typeof registration_number === "string"
        ? registration_number.trim()
        : "";

    if (
        cleanName.length > 100 ||
        cleanType.length > 100 ||
        cleanEmail.length > 254 ||
        cleanPhone.length > 30 ||
        cleanCountry.length > 100 ||
        cleanCurrency.length > 10 ||
        cleanAddress.length > 300 ||
        cleanRegistration.length > 100
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "One or more business details are too long."
        });
    }

    if (
        cleanEmail &&
        !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(cleanEmail)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please enter a valid business email address."
        });
    }

    db.run(
        `UPDATE businesses
         SET business_name = ?,
             business_type = ?,
             email = ?,
             phone = ?,
             country = ?,
             currency = ?,
             address = ?,
             registration_number = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
            cleanName,
            cleanType,
            cleanEmail || null,
            cleanPhone || null,
            cleanCountry || "Kenya",
            cleanCurrency || "KES",
            cleanAddress || null,
            cleanRegistration || null,
            businessId
        ],
        function (error) {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Business settings update error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not update business settings."
                });
            }

            if (this.changes === 0) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Business not found."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Business settings updated successfully."
            });
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

function sendDatabaseError(res, error) {
    console.error("Category database error:", error.message);
    return res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message: "A category database error occurred."
    });
}

// Check that a parent belongs to this business and is a main category.
function validateParent(businessId, parentId, callback) {
    if (parentId === null) {
        return callback(null, true);
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id FROM business_categories
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ? AND parent_id IS NULL`,
        [parentId, businessId],
        (error, row) => {
            if (error) return callback(error);
            callback(null, Boolean(row));
        }
    );
}

// Create recommended standard categories once for each business.
function seedStandardCategories(businessId, businessType, callback) {
    db.get(
        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
         FROM business_categories
         WHERE business_id = ? AND category_type = 'standard'`,
        [businessId],
        (error, result) => {
            if (error) return callback(error);

            if (result.total > 0) {
                return callback(null);
            }

            const categories =
                standardCategories[businessType] ||
                standardCategories["Retail"];

            let index = 0;

            function insertNext() {
                if (index >= categories.length) {
                    return callback(null);
                }

                const [name, description, icon] = categories[index++];

                db.run(
                    `INSERT INTO business_categories
                     (business_id, name, description, icon, category_type)
                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'standard')`,
                    [businessId, name, description, icon],
                    error => {
                        if (error) return callback(error);
                        insertNext();
                    }
                );
            }

            insertNext();
        }
    );
}

// GET /api/categories
// List categories and their product counts.
router.get("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.get(
        "SELECT business_type FROM businesses WHERE id = ?",
        [businessId],
        (error, business) => {
            if (error) return sendDatabaseError(res, error);

            if (!business) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Business not found."
                });
            }

            seedStandardCategories(
                businessId,
                business.business_type,
                seedError => {
                    if (seedError) {
                        return sendDatabaseError(res, seedError);
                    }

                    db.all(
                        `SELECT
                            c.id,
                            c.name,
                            c.description,
                            c.icon,
                            c.parent_id,
                            c.category_type,
                            c.created_at,
                            (
                                SELECT COUNT(*)
                                FROM products p
                                WHERE p.category_id = c.id
                                  AND p.business_id = c.business_id
                            ) AS product_count
                         FROM business_categories c
                         WHERE c.business_id = ?
                         ORDER BY
                            CASE WHEN c.category_type = 'standard'
                                 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
                            c.parent_id IS NOT NULL,
                            c.name COLLATE NOCASE`,
                        [businessId],
                        (listError, categories) => {
                            if (listError) {
                                return sendDatabaseError(res, listError);
                            }

                            res.json({
                                success: true,
                                categories
                            });
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// POST /api/categories
// Create a custom category or subcategory.
router.post("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const name = String(req.body.name || "").trim();
    const description = String(req.body.description || "").trim();
    const icon = String(req.body.icon || "ðŸ“").trim();
    const parentId =
        req.body.parent_id === undefined ||
        req.body.parent_id === null ||
        req.body.parent_id === ""
            ? null
            : Number(req.body.parent_id);

    if (!name || name.length > 80) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Category name is required and must be 80 characters or fewer."
        });
    }

    if (
        parentId !== null &&
        (!Number.isInteger(parentId) || parentId <= 0)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid parent category."
        });
    }

    validateParent(businessId, parentId, (error, valid) => {
        if (error) return sendDatabaseError(res, error);

        if (!valid) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Choose a valid main category belonging to your business."
            });
        }

        db.get(
            `SELECT id FROM business_categories
             WHERE business_id = ? AND name = ? AND parent_id IS ?`,
            [businessId, name, parentId],
            (checkError, existing) => {
                if (checkError) {
                    return sendDatabaseError(res, checkError);
                }

                if (existing) {
                    return res.status(409).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "This category already exists."
                    });
                }

                db.run(
                    `INSERT INTO business_categories
                     (business_id, name, description, icon, parent_id, category_type)
                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'custom')`,
                    [businessId, name, description, icon, parentId],
                    function (insertError) {
                        if (insertError) {
                            return sendDatabaseError(res, insertError);
                        }

                        res.status(201).json({
                            success: true,
                            message: "Category created successfully.",
                            category: {
                                id: this.lastID,
                                name,
                                description,
                                icon,
                                parent_id: parentId,
                                category_type: "custom",
                                product_count: 0
                            }
                        });
                    }
                );
            }
        );
    });
});

// PUT /api/categories/:id
// Only custom categories can be edited.
router.put("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const categoryId = Number(req.params.id);
    const name = String(req.body.name || "").trim();
    const description = String(req.body.description || "").trim();
    const icon = String(req.body.icon || "ðŸ“").trim();

    if (!Number.isInteger(categoryId) || categoryId <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid category."
        });
    }

    if (!name || name.length > 80) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Category name is required and must be 80 characters or fewer."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id, parent_id, category_type
         FROM business_categories
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [categoryId, businessId],
        (error, category) => {
            if (error) return sendDatabaseError(res, error);

            if (!category) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Category not found."
                });
            }

            if (category.category_type === "standard") {
                return res.status(403).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "WIN standard categories cannot be edited."
                });
            }

            db.get(
                `SELECT id FROM business_categories
                 WHERE business_id = ?
                   AND name = ?
                   AND parent_id IS ?
                   AND id != ?`,
                [businessId, name, category.parent_id, categoryId],
                (checkError, existing) => {
                    if (checkError) {
                        return sendDatabaseError(res, checkError);
                    }

                    if (existing) {
                        return res.status(409).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Another category with this name already exists."
                        });
                    }

                    db.run(
                        `UPDATE business_categories
                         SET name = ?, description = ?, icon = ?
                         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?
                           AND category_type = 'custom'`,
                        [name, description, icon, categoryId, businessId],
                        updateError => {
                            if (updateError) {
                                return sendDatabaseError(res, updateError);
                            }

                            res.json({
                                success: true,
                                message: "Category updated successfully."
                            });
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// DELETE /api/categories/:id
// Standard categories, categories in use and categories with children are protected.
router.delete("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const categoryId = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(categoryId) || categoryId <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid category."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id, category_type
         FROM business_categories
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [categoryId, businessId],
        (error, category) => {
            if (error) return sendDatabaseError(res, error);

            if (!category) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Category not found."
                });
            }

            if (category.category_type === "standard") {
                return res.status(403).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "WIN standard categories cannot be deleted."
                });
            }

            db.get(
                `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
                 FROM products
                 WHERE category_id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                [categoryId, businessId],
                (productError, productResult) => {
                    if (productError) {
                        return sendDatabaseError(res, productError);
                    }

                    if (productResult.total > 0) {
                        return res.status(409).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "This category contains products. Reassign those products before deleting it."
                        });
                    }

                    db.get(
                        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
                         FROM business_categories
                         WHERE parent_id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                        [categoryId, businessId],
                        (childError, childResult) => {
                            if (childError) {
                                return sendDatabaseError(res, childError);
                            }

                            if (childResult.total > 0) {
                                return res.status(409).json({
                                    success: false,
                                    message: "This category has subcategories. Delete or move them first."
                                });
                            }

                            db.run(
                                `DELETE FROM business_categories
                                 WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?
                                   AND category_type = 'custom'`,
                                [categoryId, businessId],
                                deleteError => {
                                    if (deleteError) {
                                        return sendDatabaseError(res, deleteError);
                                    }

                                    res.json({
                                        success: true,
                                        message: "Category deleted successfully."
                                    });
                                }
                            );
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

========== dataManagementRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const router = express.Router();

const db = require("../config/initDatabase");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

router.use(requireAuth);

// Backups are stored outside the public website directory.
const backupDirectory = path.join(
    process.cwd(),
    "private-data",
    "business-backups"
);

// Resolve the same database file used by the application.
const databasePath = process.env.WIN_DATABASE_PATH
    ? path.resolve(process.cwd(), process.env.WIN_DATABASE_PATH)
    : path.join(__dirname, "../../win-business-suite.db");

function requireBusinessOwner(req, res, next) {
    if (req.session.user.role !== "owner") {
        return res.status(403).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Only the business owner can perform this action."
        });
    }

    next();
}

function runGet(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, result) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(result || null);
        });
    });
}

function runAll(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function formatBytes(bytes) {
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;

    const units = ["KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
    let size = bytes / 1024;
    let index = 0;

    while (size >= 1024 && index < units.length - 1) {
        size /= 1024;
        index++;
    }

    return `${size.toFixed(2)} ${units[index]}`;
}

async function getStorageInformation() {
    const files = [
        databasePath,
        `${databasePath}-wal`,
        `${databasePath}-shm`,
        `${databasePath}-journal`
    ];

    let totalBytes = 0;
    const fileDetails = [];

    for (const filePath of files) {
        try {
            const stats = await fs.promises.stat(filePath);

            if (stats.isFile()) {
                totalBytes += stats.size;

                fileDetails.push({
                    file: path.basename(filePath),
                    bytes: stats.size,
                    size: formatBytes(stats.size)
                });
            }
        } catch (error) {
            if (error.code !== "ENOENT") {
                throw error;
            }
        }
    }

    return {
        totalBytes,
        totalSize: formatBytes(totalBytes),
        files: fileDetails
    };
}

async function getBusinessData(businessId) {
    const business = await runGet(
        `SELECT
            id,
            business_name,
            business_type,
            email,
            phone,
            country,
            currency,
            address,
            registration_number,
            logo_url,
            created_at
         FROM businesses
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [businessId]
    );

    if (!business) {
        throw new Error("Business not found.");
    }

    const queries = [
        [
            "users",
            `SELECT id, business_id, full_name, email, phone, role, created_at
             FROM users WHERE business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "business_categories",
            "SELECT * FROM business_categories WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "products",
            "SELECT * FROM products WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "customers",
            "SELECT * FROM customers WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "suppliers",
            "SELECT * FROM suppliers WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "purchases",
            "SELECT * FROM purchases WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "purchase_items",
            `SELECT pi.*
             FROM purchase_items pi
             INNER JOIN purchases p ON p.id = pi.purchase_id
             WHERE p.business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "supplier_payments",
            "SELECT * FROM supplier_payments WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "supplier_month_closures",
            "SELECT * FROM supplier_month_closures WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "stock_movements",
            "SELECT * FROM stock_movements WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "subscriptions",
            "SELECT * FROM subscriptions WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "subscription_payments",
            "SELECT * FROM subscription_payments WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "sales",
            "SELECT * FROM sales WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "sale_items",
            `SELECT si.*
             FROM sale_items si
             INNER JOIN sales s ON s.id = si.sale_id
             WHERE s.business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "sale_payments",
            "SELECT * FROM sale_payments WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_transactions",
            "SELECT * FROM debtor_transactions WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_transaction_items",
            `SELECT dti.*
             FROM debtor_transaction_items dti
             INNER JOIN debtor_transactions dt
                ON dt.id = dti.transaction_id
             WHERE dt.business_id = ?`,
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_payments",
            "SELECT * FROM debtor_payments WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_payment_allocations",
            "SELECT * FROM debtor_payment_allocations WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_daily_notebooks",
            "SELECT * FROM debtor_daily_notebooks WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "debtor_monthly_notebooks",
            "SELECT * FROM debtor_monthly_notebooks WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ],
        [
            "support_messages",
            "SELECT * FROM support_messages WHERE business_id = ?",
            [businessId]
        ]
    ];

    const data = {};

    for (const [name, sql, params] of queries) {
        data[name] = await runAll(sql, params);
    }

    return {
        format: "WIN Business Suite business data",
        formatVersion: 1,
        exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        businessId,
        business,
        data
    };
}

    try {
        await runGet("SELECT 1 AS connected");

        res.json({
            success: true,
            status: "connected",
            server: "online",
            database: "connected",
            checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Data management status error:", error);

        res.status(503).json({
            success: false,
            status: "unavailable",
            server: "online",
            database: "unavailable",
            message: "The database connection could not be verified."
        });
    }
});

// EXPORT THE LOGGED-IN BUSINESS'S DATA.
router.get("/export", requireBusinessOwner, async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    try {
        const exportData = await getBusinessData(businessId);

        const filename =
            `win-business-data-${businessId}-${Date.now()}.json`;

        res.setHeader(
            "Content-Type",
            "application/json; charset=utf-8"
        );

        res.setHeader(
            "Content-Disposition",
            `attachment; filename="${filename}"`
        );

        res.json(exportData);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Business data export error:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not export business data."
        });
    }
});

// CREATE A BUSINESS-SPECIFIC BACKUP.
router.post("/backups", requireBusinessOwner, async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    try {
        await fs.promises.mkdir(backupDirectory, {
            recursive: true
        });

        const backupData = await getBusinessData(businessId);

        const filename =
            `business-${businessId}-backup-${Date.now()}.json`;

        const backupPath = path.join(backupDirectory, filename);

        await fs.promises.writeFile(
            backupPath,
            JSON.stringify(backupData, null, 2),
            {
                encoding: "utf8",
                flag: "wx",
                mode: 0o600
            }
        );

        res.status(201).json({
            success: true,
            message: "Business backup created successfully.",
            backup: {
                filename,
                createdAt: backupData.exportedAt,
                size: formatBytes(
                    Buffer.byteLength(
                        JSON.stringify(backupData),
                        "utf8"
                    )
                )
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Business backup creation error:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not create a business backup."
        });
    }
});

// LIST ONLY THE LOGGED-IN BUSINESS'S BACKUPS.
router.get("/backups", requireBusinessOwner, async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const prefix = `business-${businessId}-backup-`;

    try {
        await fs.promises.mkdir(backupDirectory, {
            recursive: true
        });

        const filenames = await fs.promises.readdir(backupDirectory);
        const backups = [];

        for (const filename of filenames) {
            if (
                !filename.startsWith(prefix) ||
                !filename.endsWith(".json")
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            const backupPath = path.join(backupDirectory, filename);
            const stats = await fs.promises.stat(backupPath);

            if (!stats.isFile()) continue;

            backups.push({
                filename,
                createdAt: stats.mtime.toISOString(),
                size: formatBytes(stats.size),
                sizeBytes: stats.size
            });
        }

        backups.sort((a, b) =>
            new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt)
        );

        res.json({
            success: true,
            backups
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Business backup list error:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load business backups."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== debtorNotebookRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

const {
    ensureCustomerNotebooks,
    kenyaDateParts,
    addDays,
    getBalanceBefore,
    getTotalsBetween
} = require("../services/debtorNotebookService");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function validId(value) {
    const id = Number(value);
    return Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0 ? id : null;
}

function validYear(value) {
    const year = Number(value);

    return Number.isInteger(year) &&
        year >= 2000 &&
        year <= 2100
            ? year
            : null;
}

function validMonth(value) {
    const month = Number(value);

    return Number.isInteger(month) &&
        month >= 1 &&
        month <= 12
            ? month
            : null;
}

function validDate(value) {
    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

    if (
        date.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
        date.getUTCMonth() + 1 !== month ||
        date.getUTCDate() !== day
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return value;
}

async function findCustomer(businessId, customerId) {
    return get(
        `SELECT id, full_name
         FROM customers
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [customerId, businessId]
    );
}

function roundMoney(value) {
    return Number((Number(value) || 0).toFixed(2));
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// SHOP-WIDE REPORTS AND STATISTICS
// GET /api/debtors/reports
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/reports", async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const current = kenyaDateParts();

    const period = String(req.query.period || "daily").toLowerCase();

    if (!["daily", "monthly", "yearly"].includes(period)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Choose daily, monthly or yearly reporting."
        });
    }

    let startDate;
    let endDate;
    let reportLabel;

    if (period === "daily") {
        const date = req.query.date === undefined
            ? current.date
            : validDate(req.query.date);

        if (!date || date > current.date) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Enter a valid date that is not in the future."
            });
        }

        startDate = date;
        endDate = addDays(date, 1);
        reportLabel = date;
    }

    if (period === "monthly") {
        const year = req.query.year === undefined
            ? current.year
            : validYear(req.query.year);

        const month = req.query.month === undefined
            ? current.month
            : validMonth(req.query.month);

        if (!year || !month) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Enter a valid year and month."
            });
        }

        startDate = `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}-01`;

        const nextMonth = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 1));

        endDate = [
            nextMonth.getUTCFullYear(),
            String(nextMonth.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
            "01"
        ].join("-");

        if (startDate > current.date) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Future reporting periods are not available."
            });
        }

        reportLabel = `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }

    if (period === "yearly") {
        const year = req.query.year === undefined
            ? current.year
            : validYear(req.query.year);

        if (!year) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Enter a valid year."
            });
        }

        startDate = `${year}-01-01`;
        endDate = `${year + 1}-01-01`;

        if (startDate > current.date) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Future reporting periods are not available."
            });
        }

        reportLabel = String(year);
    }

    try {
        await ensureBusinessReportsNotebooks(businessId);

        const [
            opening,
            periodTotals,
            currentTotals,
            statusRows,
            topDebtors,
            debtorCount
        ] = await Promise.all([
            get(
                `SELECT
                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(total_amount)
                        FROM debtor_transactions
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND transaction_date < ?
                    ), 0) AS debt_before,

                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(amount)
                        FROM debtor_payments
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND payment_date < ?
                    ), 0) AS payments_before`,
                [businessId, startDate, businessId, startDate]
            ),

            get(
                `SELECT
                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(total_amount)
                        FROM debtor_transactions
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND transaction_date >= ?
                          AND transaction_date < ?
                    ), 0) AS goods_taken,

                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(amount)
                        FROM debtor_payments
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND payment_date >= ?
                          AND payment_date < ?
                    ), 0) AS payments_received`,
                [
                    businessId, startDate, endDate,
                    businessId, startDate, endDate
                ]
            ),

            get(
                `SELECT
                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(total_amount)
                        FROM debtor_transactions
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                    ), 0) AS total_debt,

                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(amount)
                        FROM debtor_payments
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                    ), 0) AS total_paid`,
                [businessId, businessId]
            ),

            all(
                `SELECT
                    CASE
                        WHEN totals.total_debt = 0
                         AND totals.total_paid = 0
                            THEN 'no_activity'

                        WHEN totals.total_debt - totals.total_paid < 0
                            THEN 'customer_credit'

                        WHEN totals.total_debt - totals.total_paid > 0
                         AND totals.total_paid > 0
                            THEN 'partially_paid'

                        WHEN totals.total_debt - totals.total_paid > 0
                            THEN 'unpaid'

                        ELSE 'paid'
                    END AS status,
                    COUNT(*) AS count

                 FROM (
                    SELECT
                        c.id,
                        COALESCE((
                            SELECT SUM(t.total_amount)
                            FROM debtor_transactions t
                            WHERE t.customer_id = c.id
                              AND t.business_id = c.business_id
                        ), 0) AS total_debt,

                        COALESCE((
                            SELECT SUM(p.amount)
                            FROM debtor_payments p
                            WHERE p.customer_id = c.id
                              AND p.business_id = c.business_id
                        ), 0) AS total_paid

                    FROM customers c
                    WHERE c.business_id = ?
                 ) totals

                 GROUP BY status`,
                [businessId]
            ),

            // TOP 10 DEBTORS
            // Uses WHERE instead of HAVING because this
            // outer query is not an aggregate query.
            all(
                `SELECT
                    debtor_totals.id,
                    debtor_totals.full_name,
                    debtor_totals.phone,
                    debtor_totals.total_debt,
                    debtor_totals.total_paid

                 FROM (
                    SELECT
                        c.id,
                        c.full_name,
                        c.phone,

                        COALESCE((
                            SELECT SUM(t.total_amount)
                            FROM debtor_transactions t
                            WHERE t.customer_id = c.id
                              AND t.business_id = c.business_id
                        ), 0) AS total_debt,

                        COALESCE((
                            SELECT SUM(p.amount)
                            FROM debtor_payments p
                            WHERE p.customer_id = c.id
                              AND p.business_id = c.business_id
                        ), 0) AS total_paid

                    FROM customers c
                    WHERE c.business_id = ?
                 ) AS debtor_totals

                 WHERE debtor_totals.total_debt -
                       debtor_totals.total_paid > 0

                 ORDER BY
                    debtor_totals.total_debt -
                    debtor_totals.total_paid DESC

                 LIMIT 10`,
                [businessId]
            ),

            get(
                `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
                 FROM customers
                 WHERE business_id = ?`,
                [businessId]
            )
        ]);

        const openingBalance = roundMoney(
            Number(opening.debt_before) -
            Number(opening.payments_before)
        );

        const goodsTaken = roundMoney(periodTotals.goods_taken);
        const paymentsReceived = roundMoney(periodTotals.payments_received);

        const closingBalance = roundMoney(
            openingBalance + goodsTaken - paymentsReceived
        );

        const totalDebt = roundMoney(currentTotals.total_debt);
        const totalPaid = roundMoney(currentTotals.total_paid);

        const outstandingDebt = roundMoney(
            Math.max(0, totalDebt - totalPaid)
        );

        const customerCredit = roundMoney(
            Math.max(0, totalPaid - totalDebt)
        );

        const statusCounts = {
            unpaid: 0,
            partially_paid: 0,
            paid: 0,
            customer_credit: 0,
            no_activity: 0
        };

        for (const row of statusRows) {
            statusCounts[row.status] = Number(row.count) || 0;
        }

        const formattedTopDebtors = topDebtors.map(debtor => ({
            id: debtor.id,
            full_name: debtor.full_name,
            phone: debtor.phone,
            total_debt: roundMoney(debtor.total_debt),
            total_paid: roundMoney(debtor.total_paid),
            balance: roundMoney(
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Number(debtor.total_debt) -
                    Number(debtor.total_paid)
                )
            )
        }));

        return res.json({
            success: true,

            report: {
                period,
                label: reportLabel,
                start_date: startDate,
                end_date_exclusive: endDate,

                opening_balance: openingBalance,
                goods_taken: goodsTaken,
                payments_received: paymentsReceived,
                closing_balance: closingBalance,

                total_debtors: Number(debtorCount.total) || 0,

                total_goods_taken_all_time: totalDebt,
                total_payments_all_time: totalPaid,

                total_outstanding_debt: outstandingDebt,
                total_customer_credit: customerCredit,

                debtor_status_counts: statusCounts,
                top_debtors: formattedTopDebtors
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Load debtor reports error:", error);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load debtor reports."
        });
    }
});

// Catch up notebooks for every customer in this business.
async function ensureBusinessReportsNotebooks(businessId) {
    const customers = await all(
        `SELECT id
         FROM customers
         WHERE business_id = ?`,
        [businessId]
    );

    for (const customer of customers) {
        await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customer.id);
    }
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET ONE DAILY NOTEBOOK SUMMARY
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/:id/notebooks/daily", async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const customerId = validId(req.params.id);
    const today = kenyaDateParts().date;
    const date = validDate(req.query.date);

    if (!customerId || !date) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid customer ID and notebook date."
        });
    }

    if (date > today) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Future notebook dates are not available."
        });
    }

    try {
        const customer = await findCustomer(businessId, customerId);

        if (!customer) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Customer not found."
            });
        }

        await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customerId);

        const notebook = await get(
            `SELECT
                id,
                opening_balance,
                debt_added,
                payments_received,
                closing_balance,
                status,
                closed_at
             FROM debtor_daily_notebooks
             WHERE business_id = ?
               AND customer_id = ?
               AND notebook_date = ?`,
            [businessId, customerId, date]
        );

        let summary = notebook;

        if (!summary) {
            const nextDate = addDays(date, 1);

            const [openingBalance, totals] = await Promise.all([
                getBalanceBefore(businessId, customerId, date),
                getTotalsBetween(
                    businessId,
                    customerId,
                    date,
                    nextDate
                )
            ]);

            const closingBalance = Number((
                openingBalance +
                totals.debtAdded -
                totals.paymentsReceived
            ).toFixed(2));

            summary = {
                id: null,
                opening_balance: openingBalance,
                debt_added: totals.debtAdded,
                payments_received: totals.paymentsReceived,
                closing_balance: closingBalance,
                status: date < today ? "closed" : "open",
                closed_at: null
            };
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            customer: {
                id: customer.id,
                full_name: customer.full_name
            },
            notebook: {
                date,
                ...summary
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Load daily notebook error:", error);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load the daily notebook."
        });
    }
});

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET ONE MONTH'S NOTEBOOK SUMMARY
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/:id/notebooks/monthly", async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const customerId = validId(req.params.id);

    const current = kenyaDateParts();

    const year = req.query.year === undefined
        ? current.year
        : validYear(req.query.year);

    const month = req.query.month === undefined
        ? current.month
        : validMonth(req.query.month);

    if (!customerId || !year || !month) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid customer ID, year and month."
        });
    }

    try {
        const customer = await findCustomer(businessId, customerId);

        if (!customer) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Customer not found."
            });
        }

        await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customerId);

        const notebook = await get(
            `SELECT
                id,
                business_id,
                customer_id,
                year,
                month,
                opening_balance,
                debt_added,
                payments_received,
                closing_balance,
                status,
                closed_at
             FROM debtor_monthly_notebooks
             WHERE business_id = ?
               AND customer_id = ?
               AND year = ?
               AND month = ?`,
            [businessId, customerId, year, month]
        );

        const summary = notebook || {
            id: null,
            business_id: businessId,
            customer_id: customerId,
            year,
            month,
            opening_balance: 0,
            debt_added: 0,
            payments_received: 0,
            closing_balance: 0,
            status: (
                year < current.year ||
                (year === current.year && month < current.month)
            ) ? "closed" : "open",
            closed_at: null
        };

        res.json({
            success: true,
            customer: {
                id: customer.id,
                full_name: customer.full_name
            },
            notebook: summary
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Load monthly notebook error:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load the monthly notebook."
        });
    }
});

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET YEARLY HISTORY, GROUPED BY MONTH
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/:id/notebooks/yearly", async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const customerId = validId(req.params.id);

    const current = kenyaDateParts();

    const year = req.query.year === undefined
        ? current.year
        : validYear(req.query.year);

    if (!customerId || !year) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid customer ID and year."
        });
    }

    try {
        const customer = await findCustomer(businessId, customerId);

        if (!customer) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Customer not found."
            });
        }

        await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customerId);

        const months = await all(
            `SELECT
                year,
                month,
                opening_balance,
                debt_added,
                payments_received,
                closing_balance,
                status,
                closed_at
             FROM debtor_monthly_notebooks
             WHERE business_id = ?
               AND customer_id = ?
               AND year = ?
             ORDER BY month ASC`,
            [businessId, customerId, year]
        );

        const totals = months.reduce((result, item) => {
            result.debt_added += Number(item.debt_added) || 0;
            result.payments_received += Number(item.payments_received) || 0;
            return result;
        }, {
            debt_added: 0,
            payments_received: 0
        });

        totals.debt_added = Number(totals.debt_added.toFixed(2));
        totals.payments_received = Number(
            totals.payments_received.toFixed(2)
        );

        const yearlySummary = {
            year,
            opening_balance: months.length
                ? Number(months[0].opening_balance) || 0
                : 0,
            debt_added: totals.debt_added,
            payments_received: totals.payments_received,
            closing_balance: months.length
                ? Number(months[months.length - 1].closing_balance) || 0
                : 0
        };

        res.json({
            success: true,
            customer: {
                id: customer.id,
                full_name: customer.full_name
            },
            year,
            summary: yearlySummary,
            months
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Load yearly notebook error:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load yearly notebook history."
        });
    }
});
