            console.error(`${label} list error:`, error);

            return sendError(
                res,
                `Unable to load ${label.toLowerCase()} records.`
            );
        }
    });

    // POST /api/finance/expenses or /income
    router.post(`/${type === "expense" ? "expenses" : "income"}`, async (req, res) => {
        try {
            const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
            const userId = getUserId(req);

            const record = normalizeRecord(req.body);

            if (record.error) {
                return sendError(res, record.error, 400);
            }

            const category = await resolveCategory(
                type,
                req.body.category,
                req.body.custom_category,
                businessId
            );

            if (!category) {
                return sendError(
                    res,
                    "Select a valid category. If you choose Other, enter its name.",
                    400
                );
            }

            const result = await run(
                `INSERT INTO ${table} (
                    business_id,
                    category,
                    description,
                    amount,
                    transaction_date,
                    payment_method,
                    reference,
                    notes,
                    created_by,
                    updated_by
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    category,
                    record.description,
                    record.amount,
                    record.transactionDate,
                    record.paymentMethod,
                    record.reference,
                    record.notes,
                    userId,
                    userId
                ]
            );

            const saved = await get(
                `SELECT *
                 FROM ${table}
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND business_id = ?`,
                [result.lastID, businessId]
            );

            return res.status(201).json({
                success: true,
                message: `${label} recorded successfully.`,
                record: saved
            });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`${label} creation error:`, error);

            return sendError(
                res,
                `Unable to record this ${label.toLowerCase()}.`
            );
        }
    });

    // GET /api/finance/expenses/:id or /income/:id
    router.get(
        `/${type === "expense" ? "expenses" : "income"}/:id`,
        async (req, res) => {
            try {
                const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
                const id = validId(req.params.id);

                if (!id) {
                    return sendError(res, "Invalid record ID.", 400);
                }

                const record = await get(
                    `SELECT *
                     FROM ${table}
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?`,
                    [id, businessId]
                );

                if (!record) {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        `${label} record not found.`,
                        404
                    );
                }

                return res.json({
                    success: true,
                    record
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`${label} details error:`, error);

                return sendError(
                    res,
                    `Unable to load ${label.toLowerCase()} details.`
                );
            }
        }
    );

    // PUT /api/finance/expenses/:id or income/:id
    router.put(
        `/${type === "expense" ? "expenses" : "income"}/:id`,
        async (req, res) => {
            try {
                const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
                const userId = getUserId(req);
                const id = validId(req.params.id);

                if (!id) {
                    return sendError(res, "Invalid record ID.", 400);
                }

                const existing = await get(
                    `SELECT id, status
                     FROM ${table}
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?`,
                    [id, businessId]
                );

                if (!existing) {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        `${label} record not found.`,
                        404
                    );
                }

                if (existing.status !== "active") {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        "Voided records cannot be edited.",
                        409
                    );
                }

                const record = normalizeRecord(req.body);

                if (record.error) {
                    return sendError(res, record.error, 400);
                }

                const category = await resolveCategory(
                    type,
                    req.body.category,
                    req.body.custom_category,
                    businessId
                );

                if (!category) {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        "Select a valid category. If you choose Other, enter its name.",
                        400
                    );
                }

                await run(
                    `UPDATE ${table}
                     SET
                        category = ?,
                        description = ?,
                        amount = ?,
                        transaction_date = ?,
                        payment_method = ?,
                        reference = ?,
                        notes = ?,
                        updated_by = ?,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?
                       AND status = 'active'`,
                    [
                        category,
                        record.description,
                        record.amount,
                        record.transactionDate,
                        record.paymentMethod,
                        record.reference,
                        record.notes,
                        userId,
                        id,
                        businessId
                    ]
                );

                const updated = await get(
                    `SELECT *
                     FROM ${table}
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?`,
                    [id, businessId]
                );

                return res.json({
                    success: true,
                    message: `${label} updated successfully.`,
                    record: updated
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`${label} update error:`, error);

                return sendError(
                    res,
                    `Unable to update this ${label.toLowerCase()}.`
                );
            }
        }
    );

    // POST /api/finance/expenses/:id/void or income/:id/void
    router.post(
        `/${type === "expense" ? "expenses" : "income"}/:id/void`,
        async (req, res) => {
            try {
                const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
                const userId = getUserId(req);
                const id = validId(req.params.id);
                const reason = cleanText(req.body.reason, 500);

                if (!id) {
                    return sendError(res, "Invalid record ID.", 400);
                }

                if (!reason) {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        "Enter a reason for voiding this record.",
                        400
                    );
                }

                const existing = await get(
                    `SELECT id, status
                     FROM ${table}
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?`,
                    [id, businessId]
                );

                if (!existing) {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        `${label} record not found.`,
                        404
                    );
                }

                if (existing.status === "voided") {
                    return sendError(
                        res,
                        "This record has already been voided.",
                        409
                    );
                }

                await run(
                    `UPDATE ${table}
                     SET
                        status = 'voided',
                        voided_by = ?,
                        voided_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                        void_reason = ?,
                        updated_by = ?,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?
                       AND status = 'active'`,
                    [
                        userId,
                        reason,
                        userId,
                        id,
                        businessId
                    ]
                );

                const voided = await get(
                    `SELECT *
                     FROM ${table}
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?`,
                    [id, businessId]
                );

                return res.json({
                    success: true,
                    message: `${label} voided successfully. It remains in your financial history.`,
                    record: voided
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`${label} void error:`, error);

                return sendError(
                    res,
                    `Unable to void this ${label.toLowerCase()}.`
                );
            }
        }
    );
}

registerRecordRoutes("expense");
registerRecordRoutes("income");

module.exports = router;

========== inventoryRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

// COMMON HELPERS
function getBusinessId(req) {
    return req.session.user.business_id;
}

function getUserId(req) {
    return req.session.user.id;
}

function validId(value) {
    const id = Number(value);
    return Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0 ? id : null;
}

function validNumber(value) {
    return value !== "" &&
        value !== null &&
        value !== undefined &&
        Number.isFinite(Number(value));
}

function rollback(res, message, status = 500) {
    db.run("ROLLBACK", error => {
        if (error) {
            console.error("Inventory rollback error:", error);
        }

        if (!res.headersSent) {
            res.status(status).json({
                success: false,
                message
            });
        }
    });
}

// GET ALL PRODUCTS
router.get("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);

    db.all(
        `SELECT
            p.id,
            p.product_name,
            p.description,
            p.selling_price,
            p.buying_price,
            p.stock_quantity,
            p.low_stock_level,
            p.category_id,
            c.name AS category_name,
            p.created_at
        FROM products p
        LEFT JOIN business_categories c
            ON c.id = p.category_id
            AND c.business_id = p.business_id
        WHERE p.business_id = ?
        ORDER BY
            COALESCE(c.name, 'Uncategorised') ASC,
            p.product_name ASC`,
        [businessId],
        (error, products) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Inventory loading error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load products."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                products
            });
        }
    );
});

// INVENTORY SUMMARY AND VALUATION
router.get("/summary", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            COUNT(*) AS total_products,
            COALESCE(SUM(stock_quantity), 0) AS total_stock_units,
            COALESCE(
                SUM(stock_quantity * buying_price), 0
            ) AS stock_cost_value,
            COALESCE(
                SUM(stock_quantity * selling_price), 0
            ) AS potential_sales_value,
            COALESCE(
                SUM(
                    CASE
                        WHEN stock_quantity <= low_stock_level
                        THEN 1
                        ELSE 0
                    END
                ), 0
            ) AS low_stock_products
        FROM products
        WHERE business_id = ?`,
        [businessId],
        (error, summary) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Inventory summary error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load inventory summary."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                summary
            });
        }
    );
});

// ADD PRODUCT
router.post("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const userId = getUserId(req);

    const {
        product_name,
        description,
        selling_price,
        buying_price,
        stock_quantity,
        low_stock_level,
        category_id
    } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof product_name !== "string" ||
        !product_name.trim()
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Product name is required."
        });
    }

    const sellingPrice = Number(selling_price);
    const buyingPrice = Number(buying_price ?? 0);
    const stockQuantity = Number(stock_quantity ?? 0);
    const lowStockLevel = Number(low_stock_level ?? 5);

    if (
        !Number.isFinite(sellingPrice) ||
        !Number.isFinite(buyingPrice) ||
        !Number.isFinite(stockQuantity) ||
        !Number.isFinite(lowStockLevel) ||
        sellingPrice < 0 ||
        buyingPrice < 0 ||
        stockQuantity < 0 ||
        lowStockLevel < 0
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Prices and stock values must be valid non-negative numbers."
        });
    }

    const cleanDescription =
        typeof description === "string"
            ? description.trim()
            : "";

    const cleanCategoryId =
        category_id === undefined ||
        category_id === null ||
        category_id === ""
            ? null
            : Number(category_id);

    if (
        cleanCategoryId !== null &&
        (!Number.isInteger(cleanCategoryId) || cleanCategoryId <= 0)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product category."
        });
    }

    function createProduct() {
        db.run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION", error => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Product transaction error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not start product creation."
                });
            }

            db.run(
                `INSERT INTO products (
                    business_id,
                    category_id,
                    product_name,
                    description,
                    selling_price,
                    buying_price,
                    stock_quantity,
                    low_stock_level
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    cleanCategoryId,
                    product_name.trim(),
                    cleanDescription,
                    sellingPrice,
                    buyingPrice,
                    stockQuantity,
                    lowStockLevel
                ],
                function (insertError) {
                    if (insertError) {
                        console.error("Product creation error:", insertError);

                        return rollback(
                            res,
                            "Could not add product."
                        );
                    }

                    const productId = this.lastID;

                    function completeCreation() {
                        db.run("COMMIT", commitError => {
                            if (commitError) {
                                console.error(
                                    "Product commit error:",
                                    commitError
                                );

                                return rollback(
                                    res,
                                    "Could not complete product creation."
                                );
                            }

                            res.status(201).json({
                                success: true,
                                message: "Product added successfully.",
                                product_id: productId
                            });
                        });
                    }

                    if (stockQuantity === 0) {
                        return completeCreation();
                    }

                    db.run(
                        `INSERT INTO stock_movements (
                            business_id,
                            product_id,
                            movement_type,
                            quantity_before,
                            quantity_changed,
                            quantity_after,
                            reason,
                            reference_type,
                            reference_id,
                            created_by
                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                        [
                            businessId,
                            productId,
                            "opening_stock",
                            0,
                            stockQuantity,
                            stockQuantity,
                            "Opening stock when product was created",
                            "product_creation",
                            productId,
                            userId
                        ],
                        movementError => {
                            if (movementError) {
                                console.error(
                                    "Opening stock recording error:",
                                    movementError
                                );

                                return rollback(
                                    res,
                                    "Could not record opening stock."
                                );
                            }

                            completeCreation();
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        });
    }

    if (cleanCategoryId === null) {
        return createProduct();
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id
         FROM business_categories
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [cleanCategoryId, businessId],
        (error, category) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Category verification error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify product category."
                });
            }

            if (!category) {
                return res.status(400).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Category not found in your business."
                });
            }

            createProduct();
        }
    );
});

// UPDATE PRODUCT DETAILS AND CURRENT QUANTITY
router.put("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const userId = getUserId(req);
    const productId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!productId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product ID."
        });
    }

    const {
        product_name,
        description,
        selling_price,
        buying_price,
        stock_quantity,
        low_stock_level,
        category_id
    } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof product_name !== "string" ||
        !product_name.trim()
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Product name is required."
        });
    }

    if (
        !validNumber(selling_price) ||
        !validNumber(buying_price) ||
        !validNumber(stock_quantity) ||
        !validNumber(low_stock_level)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter valid prices, current quantity and low-stock values."
        });
    }

    const sellingPrice = Number(selling_price);
    const buyingPrice = Number(buying_price);
    const stockQuantity = Number(stock_quantity);
    const lowStockLevel = Number(low_stock_level);

    if (
        sellingPrice < 0 ||
        buyingPrice < 0 ||
        stockQuantity < 0 ||
        lowStockLevel < 0
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Prices and stock values cannot be negative."
        });
    }

    const cleanCategoryId =
        category_id === undefined ||
        category_id === null ||
        category_id === ""
            ? null
            : Number(category_id);

    if (
        cleanCategoryId !== null &&
        (!Number.isInteger(cleanCategoryId) || cleanCategoryId <= 0)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product category."
        });
    }

    const cleanDescription =
        typeof description === "string"
            ? description.trim()
            : "";

    function updateProduct() {
        db.run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION", beginError => {
            if (beginError) {
                console.error("Product update transaction error:", beginError);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not start product update."
                });
            }

            db.get(
                `SELECT id, stock_quantity
                 FROM products
                 WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                [productId, businessId],
                (lookupError, product) => {
                    if (lookupError) {
                        console.error("Product update lookup error:", lookupError);

                        return rollback(
                            res,
                            "Could not load the product."
                        );
                    }

                    if (!product) {
                        return rollback(
                            res,
                            "Product not found.",
                            404
                        );
                    }

                    const quantityBefore = Number(product.stock_quantity);
                    const quantityChanged = stockQuantity - quantityBefore;

                    db.run(
                        `UPDATE products SET
                            category_id = ?,
                            product_name = ?,
                            description = ?,
                            selling_price = ?,
                            buying_price = ?,
                            stock_quantity = ?,
                            low_stock_level = ?
                        WHERE id = ?
                          AND business_id = ?
                          AND stock_quantity = ?`,
                        [
                            cleanCategoryId,
                            product_name.trim(),
                            cleanDescription,
                            sellingPrice,
                            buyingPrice,
                            stockQuantity,
                            lowStockLevel,
                            productId,
                            businessId,
                            quantityBefore
                        ],
                        function (updateError) {
                            if (updateError) {
                                console.error("Product update error:", updateError);

                                return rollback(
                                    res,
                                    "Could not update product."
                                );
                            }

                            if (this.changes !== 1) {
                                return rollback(
                                    res,
                                    "Product changed during this operation. Please try again.",
                                    409
                                );
                            }

                            function completeUpdate() {
                                db.run("COMMIT", commitError => {
                                    if (commitError) {
                                        console.error(
                                            "Product update commit error:",
                                            commitError
                                        );

                                        return rollback(
                                            res,
                                            "Could not complete product update."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    res.json({
                                        success: true,
                                        message: "Product and current quantity updated successfully.",
                                        stock: {
                                            quantity_before: quantityBefore,
                                            quantity_changed: quantityChanged,
                                            quantity_after: stockQuantity
                                        }
                                    });
                                });
                            }

                            if (quantityChanged === 0) {
                                return completeUpdate();
                            }

                            db.run(
                                `INSERT INTO stock_movements (
                                    business_id,
                                    product_id,
                                    movement_type,
                                    quantity_before,
                                    quantity_changed,
                                    quantity_after,
                                    reason,
                                    reference_type,
                                    reference_id,
                                    created_by
                                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                                [
                                    businessId,
                                    productId,
                                    "adjustment",
                                    quantityBefore,
                                    quantityChanged,
                                    stockQuantity,
                                    "Current quantity updated through Edit Product",
                                    "product_edit",
                                    productId,
                                    userId
                                ],
                                movementError => {
                                    if (movementError) {
                                        console.error(
                                            "Product quantity history error:",
                                            movementError
                                        );

                                        return rollback(
                                            res,
                                            "Could not record the quantity change. Product update was cancelled."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    completeUpdate();
                                }
                            );
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        });
    }

    if (cleanCategoryId === null) {
        return updateProduct();
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id
         FROM business_categories
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [cleanCategoryId, businessId],
        (error, category) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Category verification error:", error);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify product category."
                });
            }

            if (!category) {
                return res.status(400).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Category not found in your business."
                });
            }

            updateProduct();
        }
    );
});

// STOCK ADJUSTMENT ENDPOINT
// Retained for compatibility. Not displayed in the V1 interface.
router.post("/:id/adjustments", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const userId = getUserId(req);
    const productId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!productId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product ID."
        });
    }

    const quantityChange = Number(req.body.quantity_change);
    const reason =
        typeof req.body.reason === "string"
            ? req.body.reason.trim()
            : "";

    if (
        !Number.isFinite(quantityChange) ||
        quantityChange === 0
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid stock adjustment quantity."
        });
    }

    if (!reason || reason.length > 250) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "A reason of up to 250 characters is required."
        });
    }

    db.run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION", error => {
        if (error) {
            console.error("Stock adjustment transaction error:", error);

            return res.status(500).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Could not start stock adjustment."
            });
        }

        db.get(
            `SELECT id, stock_quantity
             FROM products
             WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
            [productId, businessId],
            (productError, product) => {
                if (productError) {
                    console.error("Stock product lookup error:", productError);

                    return rollback(
                        res,
                        "Could not load product."
                    );
                }

                if (!product) {
                    return rollback(
                        res,
                        "Product not found.",
                        404
                    );
                }

                const quantityBefore = Number(product.stock_quantity);
                const quantityAfter = quantityBefore + quantityChange;

                if (
                    !Number.isFinite(quantityAfter) ||
                    quantityAfter < 0
                ) {
                    return rollback(
                        res,
                        "This adjustment would make stock negative.",
                        409
                    );
                }

                db.run(
                    `UPDATE products
                     SET stock_quantity = ?
                     WHERE id = ?
                       AND business_id = ?
                       AND stock_quantity = ?`,
                    [
                        quantityAfter,
                        productId,
                        businessId,
                        quantityBefore
                    ],
                    function (updateError) {
                        if (updateError) {
                            console.error(
                                "Stock adjustment update error:",
                                updateError
                            );

                            return rollback(
                                res,
                                "Could not update stock."
                            );
                        }

                        if (this.changes !== 1) {
                            return rollback(
                                res,
                                "Stock changed during this operation. Please try again.",
                                409
                            );
                        }

                        db.run(
                            `INSERT INTO stock_movements (
                                business_id,
                                product_id,
                                movement_type,
                                quantity_before,
                                quantity_changed,
                                quantity_after,
                                reason,
                                reference_type,
                                reference_id,
                                created_by
                            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                            [
                                businessId,
                                productId,
                                "adjustment",
                                quantityBefore,
                                quantityChange,
                                quantityAfter,
                                reason,
                                "manual_adjustment",
                                productId,
                                userId
                            ],
                            movementError => {
                                if (movementError) {
                                    console.error(
                                        "Stock movement recording error:",
                                        movementError
                                    );

                                    return rollback(
                                        res,
                                        "Could not record stock adjustment."
                                    );
                                }

                                db.run("COMMIT", commitError => {
                                    if (commitError) {
                                        console.error(
                                            "Stock adjustment commit error:",
                                            commitError
                                        );

                                        return rollback(
                                            res,
                                            "Could not complete stock adjustment."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    res.json({
                                        success: true,
                                        message: "Stock adjusted successfully.",
                                        stock: {
                                            quantity_before: quantityBefore,
                                            quantity_changed: quantityChange,
                                            quantity_after: quantityAfter
                                        }
                                    });
                                });
                            }
                        );
                    }
                );
            }
        );
    });
});

// PRODUCT STOCK MOVEMENT HISTORY ENDPOINT
// Retained so existing stock history records and integrations remain available.
router.get("/:id/movements", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const productId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!productId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product ID."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id
         FROM products
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [productId, businessId],
        (productError, product) => {
            if (productError) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify product."
                });
            }

            if (!product) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Product not found."
                });
            }

            db.all(
                `SELECT
                    sm.id,
                    sm.movement_type,
                    sm.quantity_before,
                    sm.quantity_changed,
                    sm.quantity_after,
                    sm.reason,
                    sm.reference_type,
                    sm.reference_id,
                    sm.created_at,
                    u.full_name AS created_by_name
                FROM stock_movements sm
                LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = sm.created_by
                WHERE sm.business_id = ?
                  AND sm.product_id = ?
                ORDER BY sm.created_at DESC, sm.id DESC
                LIMIT 200`,
                [businessId, productId],
                (error, movements) => {
                    if (error) {
                        console.error("Movement history error:", error);

                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not load stock movement history."
                        });
                    }

                    res.json({
                        success: true,
                        movements
                    });
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// DELETE PRODUCT SAFELY
router.delete("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const productId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!productId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid product ID."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id, stock_quantity
         FROM products
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [productId, businessId],
        (productError, product) => {
            if (productError) {
                console.error("Product deletion lookup error:", productError);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify product."
                });
            }

            if (!product) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Product not found."
                });
            }

            if (Number(product.stock_quantity) > 0) {
                return res.status(409).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "This product still has stock. Update its current quantity to zero before deleting it."
                });
            }

            db.get(
                `SELECT
                    EXISTS(
                        SELECT 1 FROM stock_movements
                        WHERE product_id = ?
                    ) AS has_movements,
                    EXISTS(
                        SELECT 1 FROM debtor_transaction_items
                        WHERE product_id = ?
                    ) AS has_debtor_history,
                    EXISTS(
                        SELECT 1 FROM purchase_items
                        WHERE product_id = ?
                    ) AS has_purchase_history`,
                [productId, productId, productId],
                (historyError, history) => {
                    if (historyError) {
                        console.error(
                            "Product history check error:",
                            historyError
                        );

                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not check product history."
                        });
                    }

                    if (
                        history.has_movements ||
                        history.has_debtor_history ||
                        history.has_purchase_history
                    ) {
                        return res.status(409).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "This product has historical records and cannot be deleted. Product archiving will be provided separately."
                        });
                    }

                    db.run(
                        `DELETE FROM products
                         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                        [productId, businessId],
                        function (error) {
                            if (error) {
                                console.error(
                                    "Product deletion error:",
                                    error
                                );

                                return res.status(500).json({
                                    success: false,
                                    message: "Could not delete product."
                                });
                            }

                            if (this.changes === 0) {
                                return res.status(404).json({
                                    success: false,
                                    message: "Product not found."
                                });
                            }

                            res.json({
                                success: true,
                                message: "Product deleted successfully."
                            });
                        }
                    );
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

========== maintenanceStatusRoutes.js ==========

"use strict";

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");

const router = express.Router();

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

// Public customer-facing maintenance status.
// Never return administrator or internal audit information.
router.get("/status", async (req, res) => {
    res.set(
        "Cache-Control",
        "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private"
    );

    res.set("Pragma", "no-cache");

    try {
        const settings = await get(`
            SELECT
                maintenance_enabled,
                message,
                reason,
                expected_return_at
            FROM platform_maintenance
            WHERE id = 1
            LIMIT 1
        `);

        if (!settings) {
            return res.status(503).json({
                success: false,
                maintenance: false,
                status: "unavailable",
                message: "Maintenance status is temporarily unavailable."
            });
        }

        const maintenanceEnabled =
            Number(settings.maintenance_enabled) === 1;

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            maintenance: maintenanceEnabled,
            status: maintenanceEnabled
                ? "under_maintenance"
                : "operational",
            message: maintenanceEnabled
                ? settings.message
                : null,
            reason: maintenanceEnabled
                ? settings.reason
                : null,
            expectedReturnAt: maintenanceEnabled
                ? settings.expected_return_at
                : null
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Public maintenance status error:",
            error.message
        );

        return res.status(503).json({
            success: false,
            maintenance: false,
            status: "unavailable",
            message: "WIN Business Suite maintenance status is temporarily unavailable."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== notificationRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows || []);
        });
    });
}

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function getUserId(req) {
    return req.session.user.id;
}

function getPagination(req) {
    const page = Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1);
    const limit = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.min(50, Number.parseInt(req.query.limit, 10) || 20)
    );

    return {
        page,
        limit,
        offset: (page - 1) * limit
    };
}

function isValidDate(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "string") return false;
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return false;

    const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

    return (
        date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
        date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
        date.getUTCDate() === day
    );
}

function getDateRange(req, res) {
    const from = req.query.dateFrom || "";
    const to = req.query.dateTo || "";

    if (
        (from && !isValidDate(from)) ||
        (to && !isValidDate(to))
    ) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please provide valid dates."
        });
        return null;
    }

    if (from && to && from > to) {
        res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "The start date cannot be after the end date."
        });
        return null;
    }

    return { from, to };
}

function addDateConditions(conditions, params, range) {
    if (range.from) {
        conditions.push("created_at >= datetime(?, '-3 hours')");
        params.push(range.from);
    }

    if (range.to) {
        conditions.push("created_at < datetime(?, '+1 day', '-3 hours')");
        params.push(range.to);
    }
}

// GET ALL NOTIFICATIONS IN ONE INBOX
router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const dateRange = getDateRange(req, res);
        if (!dateRange) return;

        const userId = getUserId(req);
        const { page, limit, offset } = getPagination(req);

        const conditions = ["user_id = ?"];
        const params = [userId];

        addDateConditions(conditions, params, dateRange);

        const whereClause = conditions.join(" AND ");

        const notifications = await all(
            `SELECT
                id,
                category,
                title,
                message,
                action_label,
                action_url,
                read_at,
                created_at
             FROM user_notifications
             WHERE ${whereClause}
             ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
             LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`,
            [...params, limit, offset]
        );

        const counts = await get(
            `SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS total,
                COALESCE(SUM(
                    CASE WHEN read_at IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END
                ), 0) AS unread,
                COALESCE(SUM(
                    CASE WHEN read_at IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END
                ), 0) AS read
             FROM user_notifications
             WHERE ${whereClause}`,
            params
        );

        const total = counts.total || 0;

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            notifications,
            counts: {
                total,
                unread: counts.unread || 0,
                read: counts.read || 0
            },
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
            }
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Notification lookup error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load your notifications."
        });
    }
});

// GET UNREAD COUNT FOR THE DASHBOARD BELL
router.get("/unread-count", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const result = await get(
            `SELECT COUNT(*) AS unread_count
             FROM user_notifications
             WHERE user_id = ?
               AND read_at IS NULL`,
            [getUserId(req)]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            unread_count: result.unread_count
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Unread count error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load your notification count."
        });
    }
});

// CLAIM NEXT ONE-TIME POPUP
router.post("/popup-queue/claim", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const userId = getUserId(req);

        const popup = await get(
            `UPDATE user_notifications
             SET popup_shown_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
             WHERE id = (
                 SELECT id
                 FROM user_notifications
                 WHERE user_id = ?
                   AND read_at IS NULL
                   AND popup_shown_at IS NULL
                   AND popup_dismissed_at IS NULL
                 ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
                 LIMIT 1
             )
               AND user_id = ?
               AND popup_shown_at IS NULL
             RETURNING
                id,
                category,
                title,
                message,
                action_label,
                action_url,
                created_at`,
            [userId, userId]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            popup: popup || null
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Popup queue error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not load your next notification."
        });
    }
});

// CLOSE POPUP WITH X WITHOUT MARKING AS READ
router.patch("/:id/popup-dismiss", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const notificationId = Number.parseInt(req.params.id, 10);
        const userId = getUserId(req);

        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(notificationId) || notificationId <= 0) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const result = await run(
            `UPDATE user_notifications
             SET popup_dismissed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
             WHERE id = ?
               AND user_id = ?
               AND popup_shown_at IS NOT NULL
               AND popup_dismissed_at IS NULL
               AND read_at IS NULL`,
            [notificationId, userId]
        );

        if (result.changes === 0) {
            const notification = await get(
                `SELECT id FROM user_notifications
                 WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?`,
                [notificationId, userId]
            );

            if (!notification) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Notification not found."
                });
            }
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Popup closed. Notification remains in your inbox."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Popup dismissal error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not close the notification popup."
        });
    }
});

// MARK ONE NOTIFICATION AS READ
router.patch("/:id/read", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const notificationId = Number.parseInt(req.params.id, 10);
        const userId = getUserId(req);

        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(notificationId) || notificationId <= 0) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Invalid notification ID."
            });
        }

        const result = await run(
            `UPDATE user_notifications
             SET
                read_at = COALESCE(read_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),
                popup_dismissed_at = COALESCE(
                    popup_dismissed_at,
                    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
             WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?`,
            [notificationId, userId]
        );

        if (result.changes === 0) {
            const notification = await get(
                `SELECT id FROM user_notifications
                 WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?`,
                [notificationId, userId]
            );

            if (!notification) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Notification not found."
                });
            }
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Notification marked as read."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Mark notification read error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not update your notification."
        });
    }
});

// MARK ALL NOTIFICATIONS AS READ
router.post("/read-all", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const result = await run(
            `UPDATE user_notifications
             SET
                read_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                popup_dismissed_at = COALESCE(
                    popup_dismissed_at,
                    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
             WHERE user_id = ?
               AND read_at IS NULL`,
            [getUserId(req)]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            updated: result.changes,
            message: "All notifications have been marked as read."
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Mark all notifications error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Could not mark all notifications as read."
        });
    }
});

========== passwordResetRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");
const db = require("../config/database");

const {
    createEmailToken,
    consumeEmailToken
} = require("../services/emailTokenService");

const { sendEmail } = require("../services/emailService");
const { checkLimit } = require("../services/emailRateLimitService");

const router = express.Router();

const RESET_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 10;

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row || null);
        });
    });
}

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(this);
        });
    });
}

function recordSecurityEvent(userId, email, eventType, req) {
    db.run(
        `INSERT INTO email_security_events (
            user_id, email, event_type, ip_address, user_agent
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
            userId || null,
            email || null,
            eventType,
            req.ip || null,
            req.get("user-agent") || null
        ],
        error => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Security event log failed:", error.message);
            }
        }
    );
}

async function checkRequestLimit(identifier, action, settings) {
    const result = await checkLimit({
        identifier,
        action,
        ...settings
    });

    return result;
}

function escapeHtml(value) {
    const replacements = {
        "&": "&amp;",
        "<": "&lt;",
        ">": "&gt;",
        '"': "&quot;",
        "'": "&#39;"
    };

    return String(value || "").replace(
        /[&<>"']/g,
        character => replacements[character]
    );
}

async function sendPasswordResetEmail(user, token) {
    const appUrl = (process.env.APP_URL || "http://localhost:3000")
        .replace(/\/+$/, "");

    const resetUrl =
        `${appUrl}/password-reset.html?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;

    const subject = "Reset your password - WIN Business Suite";
    const safeName = escapeHtml(user.full_name || "there");

    const text = [
        `Hello ${user.full_name || "there"},`,
        "",
        "A password reset was requested for your WIN Business Suite account.",
        "Open the link below to choose a new password:",
        resetUrl,
        "",
        "This link expires in 10 minutes and can only be used once.",
        "If you did not request this, ignore this email. Your password will not change.",
        "",
        "WIN Business Suite Team"
    ].join("\n");

    const html = `
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="en">
        <head>
            <meta charset="UTF-8">
            <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
            <title>Reset your password</title>
        </head>
        <body style="margin:0;padding:30px 12px;background:#f3f6fb;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#17233c;">
            <div style="max-width:560px;margin:0 auto;background:#ffffff;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid #e4eaf2;">
                <div style="background:#101d3a;padding:30px;text-align:center;">
                    <h1 style="margin:0;color:#ffffff;font-size:27px;">WIN <span style="color:#25c4e8;">Business Suite</span></h1>
                    <p style="margin:10px 0 0;color:#d8e5f7;font-size:13px;">Manage Everything. Grow Smarter.</p>
                </div>
                <div style="padding:32px 28px;">
                    <h2 style="color:#101d3a;">Reset your password</h2>
                    <p>Hello ${safeName},</p>
                    <p>We received a request to reset your WIN Business Suite password. Use the button below to choose a new password.</p>
                    <div style="text-align:center;margin:30px 0;">
                        <a href="${resetUrl}" style="display:inline-block;background:#13b9df;color:#ffffff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:bold;padding:15px 25px;border-radius:8px;">
                            Reset Password
                        </a>
                    </div>
                    <p style="font-size:13px;color:#58677e;">This link expires in <strong>10 minutes</strong> and can only be used once.</p>
                    <p style="font-size:13px;color:#58677e;">If you did not request this, ignore this email. Your password will not change.</p>
                </div>
                <div style="background:#f3f6fb;padding:20px;text-align:center;color:#68758a;font-size:12px;">
                    WIN Business Suite Team<br>
                    Bori Technologies Group
                </div>
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
    `;

    const result = await sendEmail({
        to: user.email,
        subject,
        text,
        html
    });

    await run(
        `INSERT INTO email_delivery_logs (
            user_id, recipient_email, email_type, subject,
            status, provider_message_id
        ) VALUES (?, ?, 'password_reset', ?, 'sent', ?)`,
        [user.id, user.email, subject, result.messageId || null]
    );
}

// REQUEST PASSWORD RESET
router.post("/password-reset/request", async (req, res) => {
    const genericResponse = {
        success: true,
        message: "If a verified account exists for that email, a password reset link will be sent. The link expires in 10 minutes."
    };

    try {
        const ipResult = await checkRequestLimit(
            req.ip || "unknown",
            "password-reset-request:ip",
            {
                maxRequests: 5,
                windowMinutes: 15,
                blockMinutes: 30
            }
        );

        if (!ipResult.allowed) {
            res.set("Retry-After", String(ipResult.retryAfterSeconds));
            return res.status(429).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Too many reset requests. Please wait before trying again.",
                retryAfterSeconds: ipResult.retryAfterSeconds
            });
        }

        const email = typeof req.body.email === "string"
            ? req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase()
            : "";

        if (!email || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
            return res.json(genericResponse);
        }

        const emailResult = await checkRequestLimit(
            email,
            "password-reset-request:email",
            {
                maxRequests: 3,
                windowMinutes: 15,
                blockMinutes: 30
            }
        );

        if (!emailResult.allowed) {
            res.set("Retry-After", String(emailResult.retryAfterSeconds));
            return res.status(429).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Too many reset requests. Please wait before trying again.",
                retryAfterSeconds: emailResult.retryAfterSeconds
            });
        }

        const user = await get(
            `SELECT id, full_name, email, email_verified
             FROM users
             WHERE email = ?`,
            [email]
        );

        // Do not reveal whether an email address has an account.
        if (!user || Number(user.email_verified) !== 1) {
            return res.json(genericResponse);
        }

        const token = await createEmailToken(
            user.id,
            "password_reset",
            RESET_EXPIRY_MINUTES
        );

        try {
            await sendPasswordResetEmail(user, token);

            recordSecurityEvent(
                user.id,
                user.email,
                "password_reset_requested",
                req
            );
        } catch (emailError) {
            console.error("Password reset email failed:", emailError.message);

            await run(
                `INSERT INTO email_delivery_logs (
                    user_id, recipient_email, email_type, subject,
                    status, error_message
                ) VALUES (?, ?, 'password_reset', ?, 'failed', ?)`,
                [
                    user.id,
                    user.email,
                    "Reset your password - WIN Business Suite",
                    String(emailError.message).slice(0, 500)
                ]
            );
        }

        return res.json(genericResponse);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Password reset request error:", error.message);

        return res.status(503).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Password reset is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later."
        });
    }
});

// COMPLETE PASSWORD RESET
router.post("/password-reset/complete", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const ipResult = await checkRequestLimit(
            req.ip || "unknown",
            "password-reset-complete:ip",
            {
                maxRequests: 10,
                windowMinutes: 15,
                blockMinutes: 15
            }
        );

        if (!ipResult.allowed) {
            res.set("Retry-After", String(ipResult.retryAfterSeconds));
            return res.status(429).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Too many attempts. Please wait before trying again.",
                retryAfterSeconds: ipResult.retryAfterSeconds
            });
        }

        const { token, password } = req.body;

        if (
            typeof token !== "string" ||
            typeof password !== "string"
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "The reset link and new password are required."
            });
        }

        if (password.length < 12 || password.length > 128) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Your new password must contain 12 to 128 characters."
            });
        }

        const userId = await consumeEmailToken(
            token,
            "password_reset"
        );

        if (!userId) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "This password reset link is invalid, expired, or already used. Please request a new link."
            });
        }

        const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);

        const updateResult = await run(
            `UPDATE users
             SET password_hash = ?
             WHERE id = ?
             AND email_verified = 1`,
            [passwordHash, userId]
        );

        if (updateResult.changes !== 1) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "The account could not be updated. Please request a new reset link."
            });
        }

        // Invalidate outstanding login confirmation links after a password change.
        await run(
            `UPDATE email_tokens
             SET used_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
             WHERE user_id = ?
             AND purpose = 'login_confirmation'
             AND used_at IS NULL`,
            [userId]
        );

        const user = await get(
            `SELECT id, full_name, email
             FROM users
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [userId]
        );

        recordSecurityEvent(
            user.id,
            user.email,
            "password_changed",
            req
        );

        // Password change succeeds even if the notification email fails.
        try {
            await sendPasswordChangedNotice(user);
        } catch (noticeError) {
            console.error(
                "Password-change notification failed:",
                noticeError.message
            );

            try {
                await run(
                    `INSERT INTO email_delivery_logs (
                        user_id, recipient_email, email_type, subject,
                        status, error_message
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, 'password_changed', ?, 'failed', ?)`,
                    [
                        user.id,
                        user.email,
                        "Your WIN Business Suite password was changed",
                        String(noticeError.message).slice(0, 500)
                    ]
                );
            } catch (logError) {
                console.error("Notification log failed:", logError.message);
            }
        }

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Your password has been changed successfully. You can now log in using your new password."
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Password reset completion error:", error.message);

        return res.status(500).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Your password could not be changed. Please try again."
        });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== purchaseRoutes.js ==========
const express = require("express");
const crypto = require("crypto");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

// VALIDATE A REAL YYYY-MM-DD DATE
function isValidDate(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "string") return false;

    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
        return false;
    }

    const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

    return (
        date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
        date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
        date.getUTCDate() === day
    );
}

// LOAD PRODUCTS AND SUPPLIERS FOR THE PURCHASE FORM
router.get("/options", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.all(
        `SELECT id, product_name, buying_price, stock_quantity
         FROM products
         WHERE business_id = ?
         ORDER BY product_name ASC`,
        [businessId],
        (productError, products) => {
            if (productError) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load products."
                });
            }

            db.all(
                `SELECT id, supplier_name, phone
                 FROM suppliers
                 WHERE business_id = ?
                 ORDER BY supplier_name ASC`,
                [businessId],
                (supplierError, suppliers) => {
                    if (supplierError) {
                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not load suppliers."
                        });
                    }

                    res.json({
                        success: true,
                        products,
                        suppliers
                    });
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// GET PURCHASE HISTORY
router.get("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.all(
        `SELECT
            p.id,
            p.purchase_number,
            p.total_amount,
            p.purchase_date,
            p.notes,
            s.supplier_name
         FROM purchases p
         LEFT JOIN suppliers s ON s.id = p.supplier_id
         WHERE p.business_id = ?
         ORDER BY p.id DESC`,
        [businessId],
        (error, purchases) => {
            if (error) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load purchase history."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                purchases
            });
        }
    );
});

// GET PURCHASE DETAILS
router.get("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const purchaseId = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(purchaseId) || purchaseId <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid purchase ID."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            p.*,
            s.supplier_name,
            s.phone AS supplier_phone
         FROM purchases p
         LEFT JOIN suppliers s ON s.id = p.supplier_id
         WHERE p.id = ? AND p.business_id = ?`,
        [purchaseId, businessId],
        (error, purchase) => {
            if (error) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load purchase."
                });
            }

            if (!purchase) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Purchase not found."
                });
            }

            db.all(
                `SELECT
                    id,
                    product_id,
                    product_name,
                    quantity,
                    unit_cost,
                    line_total
                 FROM purchase_items
                 WHERE purchase_id = ?
                 ORDER BY id ASC`,
                [purchaseId],
                (itemsError, items) => {
                    if (itemsError) {
                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not load purchase items."
                        });
                    }

                    res.json({
                        success: true,
                        purchase,
                        items
                    });
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// CREATE A PURCHASE AND INCREASE STOCK
router.post("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const userId = req.session.user.id;

    const supplierId =
        req.body.supplier_id === undefined ||
        req.body.supplier_id === null ||
        req.body.supplier_id === ""
            ? null
            : Number(req.body.supplier_id);

    const notes =
        typeof req.body.notes === "string"
            ? req.body.notes.trim()
            : "";

    const purchaseDate =
        req.body.purchase_date === undefined ||
        req.body.purchase_date === null ||
        req.body.purchase_date === ""
            ? null
            : req.body.purchase_date;

    if (
        purchaseDate !== null &&
        !isValidDate(purchaseDate)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid purchase date."
        });
    }

    const items = req.body.items;

    if (
        supplierId !== null &&
        (!Number.isInteger(supplierId) || supplierId <= 0)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid supplier."
        });
    }

    if (
        !Array.isArray(items) ||
        items.length === 0 ||
        items.length > 100
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Add between 1 and 100 purchase items."
        });
    }

    const cleanItems = [];
    const seenProducts = new Set();

    for (const item of items) {
        const productId = Number(item.product_id);
        const quantity = Number(item.quantity);
        const unitCost = Number(item.unit_cost);

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(productId) ||
            productId <= 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(quantity) ||
            quantity <= 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(unitCost) ||
            unitCost < 0
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Each item needs a valid product, quantity and buying price."
            });
        }

        if (seenProducts.has(productId)) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "A product can only appear once in a purchase."
            });
        }

        seenProducts.add(productId);

        const lineTotal = quantity * unitCost;

        if (!Number.isFinite(lineTotal) || lineTotal > 1000000000000) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Purchase item total is too large."
            });
        }

        cleanItems.push({
            productId,
            quantity,
            unitCost,
            lineTotal
        });
    }

    const totalAmount = cleanItems.reduce(
        (total, item) => total + item.lineTotal,
        0
    );

    if (
        !Number.isFinite(totalAmount) ||
        totalAmount > 1000000000000
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Purchase total is invalid."
        });
    }

    const productIds = cleanItems.map(item => item.productId);
    const placeholders = productIds.map(() => "?").join(", ");

    const purchaseNumber =
        "PUR-" +
        Date.now() +
        "-" +
