        crypto.randomBytes(3).toString("hex").toUpperCase();

    const createPurchase = () => {
        db.all(
            `SELECT id, product_name
             FROM products
             WHERE business_id = ?
             AND id IN (${placeholders})`,
            [businessId, ...productIds],
            (productError, products) => {
                if (productError) {
                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Could not verify purchase products."
                    });
                }

                if (products.length !== cleanItems.length) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "One or more products do not belong to your business."
                    });
                }

                const productMap = new Map(
                    products.map(product => [product.id, product])
                );

                db.serialize(() => {
                    db.run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION", (beginError) => {
                        if (beginError) {
                            return res.status(500).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "Could not start purchase transaction."
                            });
                        }

                        let finished = false;

                        const rollback = (status, message) => {
                            if (finished) return;
                            finished = true;

                            db.run("ROLLBACK", () => {
                                res.status(status).json({
                                    success: false,
                                    message
                                });
                            });
                        };

                        // CHECK WHETHER THE SUPPLIER'S MONTH IS CLOSED
                        const savePurchase = () => {
                            db.run(
                                `INSERT INTO purchases (
                                    business_id,
                                    supplier_id,
                                    purchase_number,
                                    total_amount,
                                    purchase_date,
                                    notes,
                                    created_by
                                ) VALUES (
                                    ?, ?, ?, ?,
                                    COALESCE(?, date('now', 'localtime')),
                                    ?, ?
                                )`,
                                [
                                    businessId,
                                    supplierId,
                                    purchaseNumber,
                                    totalAmount,
                                    purchaseDate,
                                    notes,
                                    userId
                                ],
                                function (purchaseError) {
                                    if (purchaseError) {
                                        return rollback(
                                            500,
                                            "Could not save purchase."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    const purchaseId = this.lastID;
                                    let itemIndex = 0;

                                    const saveNextItem = () => {
                                        if (itemIndex >= cleanItems.length) {
                                            if (finished) return;

                                            db.run("COMMIT", (commitError) => {
                                                if (commitError) {
                                                    return rollback(
                                                        500,
                                                        "Could not complete purchase."
                                                    );
                                                }

                                                finished = true;

                                                res.status(201).json({
                                                    success: true,
                                                    message: "Purchase recorded and stock updated successfully.",
                                                    purchase_id: purchaseId,
                                                    purchase_number: purchaseNumber,
                                                    total_amount: totalAmount
                                                });
                                            });

                                            return;
                                        }

                                        const item = cleanItems[itemIndex];
                                        const product = productMap.get(item.productId);

                                        db.run(
                                            `INSERT INTO purchase_items (
                                                purchase_id,
                                                product_id,
                                                product_name,
                                                quantity,
                                                unit_cost,
                                                line_total
                                            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                                            [
                                                purchaseId,
                                                item.productId,
                                                product.product_name,
                                                item.quantity,
                                                item.unitCost,
                                                item.lineTotal
                                            ],
                                            (itemError) => {
                                                if (itemError) {
                                                    return rollback(
                                                        500,
                                                        "Could not save purchase items."
                                                    );
                                                }

                                                db.run(
                                                    `UPDATE products
                                                     SET stock_quantity = stock_quantity + ?
                                                     WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                                                    [
                                                        item.quantity,
                                                        item.productId,
                                                        businessId
                                                    ],
                                                    function (stockError) {
                                                        if (
                                                            stockError ||
                                                            this.changes !== 1
                                                        ) {
                                                            return rollback(
                                                                500,
                                                                "Could not update product stock."
                                                            );
                                                        }

                                                        itemIndex++;
                                                        saveNextItem();
                                                    }
                                                );
                                            }
                                        );
                                    };

                                    saveNextItem();
                                }
                            );
                        };

                        if (supplierId === null) {
                            return savePurchase();
                        }

                        const selectedDate =
                            purchaseDate ||
                            null;

                        const checkClosedMonth = (dateToCheck) => {
                            db.get(
                                `SELECT id
                                 FROM supplier_month_closures
                                 WHERE business_id = ?
                                   AND supplier_id = ?
                                   AND year = CAST(strftime('%Y', ?) AS INTEGER)
                                   AND month = CAST(strftime('%m', ?) AS INTEGER)`,
                                [
                                    businessId,
                                    supplierId,
                                    dateToCheck,
                                    dateToCheck
                                ],
                                (closureError, closure) => {
                                    if (closureError) {
                                        return rollback(
                                            500,
                                            "Could not check the supplier's month status."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    if (closure) {
                                        return rollback(
                                            409,
                                            "This supplier's month is closed. You cannot add purchases to it."
                                        );
                                    }

                                    savePurchase();
                                }
                            );
                        };

                        if (selectedDate) {
                            return checkClosedMonth(selectedDate);
                        }

                        db.get(
                            "SELECT date('now', 'localtime') AS today",
                            [],
                            (dateError, result) => {
                                if (dateError || !result) {
                                    return rollback(
                                        500,
                                        "Could not determine today's date."
                                    );
                                }

                                checkClosedMonth(result.today);
                            }
                        );
                    });
                });
            }
        );
    };

    // VERIFY SUPPLIER BELONGS TO THIS BUSINESS
    if (supplierId === null) {
        return createPurchase();
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id
         FROM suppliers
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [supplierId, businessId],
        (supplierError, supplier) => {
            if (supplierError) {
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify supplier."
                });
            }

            if (!supplier) {
                return res.status(400).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Supplier not found in your business."
                });
            }

            createPurchase();
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

========== salesRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const crypto = require("crypto");

const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");
const {
    ensureCustomerNotebooks,
    kenyaDateParts
} = require("../services/debtorNotebookService");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

const PAYMENT_METHODS = [
    "cash",
    "mpesa",
    "bank_transfer",
    "other"
];

function run(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.run(sql, params, function (error) {
            if (error) return reject(error);

            resolve({
                lastID: this.lastID,
                changes: this.changes
            });
        });
    });
}

function get(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.get(sql, params, (error, row) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(row);
        });
    });
}

function all(sql, params = []) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        db.all(sql, params, (error, rows) => {
            if (error) return reject(error);
            resolve(rows);
        });
    });
}

function roundMoney(value) {
    return Math.round((Number(value) + Number.EPSILON) * 100) / 100;
}

function validId(value) {
    const id = Number(value);

    return Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0
        ? id
        : null;
}

function cleanText(value, maxLength = 250) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
        return "";
    }

    return String(value).trim().slice(0, maxLength);
}

function validAmount(value) {
    const amount = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(amount) && amount >= 0
        ? roundMoney(amount)
        : null;
}

function getBusinessId(req) {
    return Number(req.session.user.business_id);
}

function getUserId(req) {
    return Number(req.session.user.id);
}

function createSaleNumber(date) {
    const datePart = date.replace(/-/g, "");
    const randomPart = crypto.randomBytes(5).toString("hex").toUpperCase();

    return `WIN-${datePart}-${randomPart}`;
}

async function rollbackTransaction() {
    try {
        await run("ROLLBACK");
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Sale rollback error:", error.message);
    }
}

function sendError(res, message, status = 500) {
    return res.status(status).json({
        success: false,
        message
    });
}

function calculateDebtorBalance(totalDebt, totalPaid) {
    return roundMoney(
        Number(totalDebt || 0) - Number(totalPaid || 0)
    );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/sales
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const businessId = getBusinessId(req);

        const page = Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(req.query.page, 10) || 1);
        const limit = Math.min(
            100,
            Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(req.query.limit, 10) || 25)
        );

        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const conditions = ["s.business_id = ?"];
        const params = [businessId];

        const startDate = cleanText(req.query.start_date, 10);
        const endDate = cleanText(req.query.end_date, 10);
        const search = cleanText(req.query.search, 100);

        if (startDate) {
            if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(startDate)) {
                return sendError(res, "Invalid start date.", 400);
            }

            conditions.push("s.sale_date >= ?");
            params.push(startDate);
        }

        if (endDate) {
            if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDate)) {
                return sendError(res, "Invalid end date.", 400);
            }

            conditions.push("s.sale_date <= ?");
            params.push(endDate);
        }

        if (search) {
            conditions.push(`(
                s.sale_number LIKE ?
                OR s.notes LIKE ?
                OR c.full_name LIKE ?
                OR c.phone LIKE ?
            )`);

            params.push(
                `%${search}%`,
                `%${search}%`,
                `%${search}%`,
                `%${search}%`
            );
        }

        const where = conditions.join(" AND ");

        const count = await get(
            `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total
             FROM sales s
             LEFT JOIN customers c
                ON c.id = s.customer_id
               AND c.business_id = s.business_id
             WHERE ${where}`,
            params
        );

        const sales = await all(
            `SELECT
                s.id,
                s.sale_number,
                s.customer_id,
                c.full_name AS customer_name,
                c.phone AS customer_phone,
                s.sale_date,
                s.subtotal,
                s.discount_amount,
                s.total_amount,
                s.amount_paid,
                s.balance_due,
                s.payment_status,
                s.status,
                s.notes,
                s.created_at,
                u.full_name AS cashier_name
             FROM sales s
             LEFT JOIN customers c
                ON c.id = s.customer_id
               AND c.business_id = s.business_id
             LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = s.created_by
             WHERE ${where}
             ORDER BY s.id DESC
             LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`,
            [...params, limit, offset]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            sales,
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total: count.total,
                total_pages: Math.ceil(count.total / limit)
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Sales list error:", error);

        return sendError(res, "Unable to load sales.");
    }
});

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/sales/summary
// Sales, transactions, cost of goods and gross profit
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/summary", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
        const today = kenyaDateParts().date;

        const startDate = cleanText(
            req.query.start_date || today,
            10
        );

        const endDate = cleanText(
            req.query.end_date || today,
            10
        );

        if (
            !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(startDate) ||
            !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDate) ||
            startDate > endDate
        ) {
            return sendError(res, "Invalid date range.", 400);
        }

        const summary = await get(
            `SELECT
                COUNT(*) AS total_sales,

                COALESCE(SUM(s.subtotal), 0) AS subtotal,

                COALESCE(SUM(s.discount_amount), 0) AS discounts,

                COALESCE(SUM(s.total_amount), 0) AS total_revenue,

                COALESCE(SUM(s.amount_paid), 0) AS amount_received,

                COALESCE(SUM(s.balance_due), 0) AS outstanding_balance,

                COALESCE((
                    SELECT SUM(
                        si.line_total -
                        (si.quantity * COALESCE(si.unit_cost, 0))
                    )
                    FROM sale_items si
                    INNER JOIN sales linked_sale
                        ON linked_sale.id = si.sale_id
                    WHERE linked_sale.business_id = ?
                      AND linked_sale.sale_date >= ?
                      AND linked_sale.sale_date <= ?
                      AND linked_sale.status = 'completed'
                ), 0) AS gross_profit,

                COALESCE((
                    SELECT SUM(
                        si.quantity * COALESCE(si.unit_cost, 0)
                    )
                    FROM sale_items si
                    INNER JOIN sales linked_sale
                        ON linked_sale.id = si.sale_id
                    WHERE linked_sale.business_id = ?
                      AND linked_sale.sale_date >= ?
                      AND linked_sale.sale_date <= ?
                      AND linked_sale.status = 'completed'
                ), 0) AS cost_of_goods

             FROM sales s
             WHERE s.business_id = ?
               AND s.sale_date >= ?
               AND s.sale_date <= ?
               AND s.status = 'completed'`,
            [
                businessId,
                startDate,
                endDate,
                businessId,
                startDate,
                endDate,
                businessId,
                startDate,
                endDate
            ]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            period: {
                start_date: startDate,
                end_date: endDate
            },
            summary: {
                total_sales: Number(summary.total_sales || 0),
                subtotal: roundMoney(summary.subtotal),
                discounts: roundMoney(summary.discounts),
                total_revenue: roundMoney(summary.total_revenue),
                amount_received: roundMoney(summary.amount_received),
                outstanding_balance: roundMoney(
                    summary.outstanding_balance
                ),
                cost_of_goods: roundMoney(summary.cost_of_goods),
                gross_profit: roundMoney(summary.gross_profit)
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Sales summary error:", error);

        return sendError(res, "Unable to load sales summary.");
    }
});

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/sales/:id
// --------------------------------------------------

router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
        const saleId = validId(req.params.id);

        if (!saleId) {
            return sendError(res, "Invalid sale ID.", 400);
        }

        const sale = await get(
            `SELECT
                s.*,
                c.full_name AS customer_name,
                c.phone AS customer_phone,
                u.full_name AS cashier_name
             FROM sales s
             LEFT JOIN customers c
                ON c.id = s.customer_id
               AND c.business_id = s.business_id
             LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = s.created_by
             WHERE s.id = ?
               AND s.business_id = ?`,
            [saleId, businessId]
        );

        if (!sale) {
            return sendError(res, "Sale not found.", 404);
        }

        const items = await all(
            `SELECT
                id,
                product_id,
                product_name,
                quantity,
                unit_price,
                unit_cost,
                line_subtotal,
                discount_amount,
                line_total
             FROM sale_items
             WHERE sale_id = ?
             ORDER BY id`,
            [saleId]
        );

        const originalPayments = await all(
            `SELECT
                id,
                payment_date,
                amount,
                payment_method,
                reference,
                notes,
                'sale' AS payment_source
             FROM sale_payments
             WHERE sale_id = ?
               AND business_id = ?
             ORDER BY id`,
            [saleId, businessId]
        );

        const laterPayments = await all(
            `SELECT
                dp.id,
                dp.payment_date,
                dpa.amount,
                dp.payment_method,
                dp.reference,
                dp.notes,
                'debtor' AS payment_source
             FROM debtor_payment_allocations dpa
             JOIN debtor_payments dp
               ON dp.id = dpa.payment_id
              AND dp.business_id = dpa.business_id
             WHERE dpa.sale_id = ?
               AND dpa.business_id = ?
             ORDER BY dp.id`,
            [saleId, businessId]
        );

        return res.json({
            success: true,
            sale,
            items,
            payments: [...originalPayments, ...laterPayments]
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Sale details error:", error);

        return sendError(res, "Unable to load sale details.");
    }
});

// --------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/sales
// Create a sale and automatically create debtor
// records for any remaining unpaid balance.
// --------------------------------------------------

router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
    const businessId = getBusinessId(req);
    const userId = getUserId(req);

    let transactionStarted = false;

    try {
        const customerId = req.body.customer_id
            ? validId(req.body.customer_id)
            : null;

        if (req.body.customer_id && !customerId) {
            return sendError(res, "Invalid customer ID.", 400);
        }

        const notes = cleanText(req.body.notes, 1000);
        const items = req.body.items;
        const payments = req.body.payments ?? [];

        if (
            !Array.isArray(items) ||
            items.length === 0 ||
            items.length > 100
        ) {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "Add between 1 and 100 sale items.",
                400
            );
        }

        if (!Array.isArray(payments) || payments.length > 10) {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "Add between 0 and 10 payment entries.",
                400
            );
        }

        const seenProducts = new Set();

        for (const item of items) {
            const productId = validId(item?.product_id);
            const quantity = Number(item?.quantity);

            if (!productId) {
                return sendError(res, "Invalid product ID.", 400);
            }

            if (seenProducts.has(productId)) {
                return sendError(
                    res,
                    "A product can only appear once in a sale.",
                    400
                );
            }

            seenProducts.add(productId);

            if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0) {
                return sendError(
                    res,
                    "Every product quantity must be greater than zero.",
                    400
                );
            }
        }

        const normalizedPayments = [];

        for (const payment of payments) {
            const amount = validAmount(payment?.amount);
            const method = cleanText(
                payment?.payment_method,
                30
            ).toLowerCase();

            if (amount === null || amount <= 0) {
                return sendError(
                    res,
                    "Every payment amount must be greater than zero.",
                    400
                );
            }

            if (!PAYMENT_METHODS.includes(method)) {
                return sendError(
                    res,
                    "Invalid payment method.",
                    400
                );
            }

            normalizedPayments.push({
                amount,
                method,
                reference: cleanText(payment.reference, 100),
                notes: cleanText(payment.notes, 250)
            });
        }

        const requestedDiscount = validAmount(
            req.body.discount_amount ?? 0
        );

        if (requestedDiscount === null) {
            return sendError(res, "Invalid discount amount.", 400);
        }

        await run("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION");
        transactionStarted = true;

        const saleItems = [];
        let subtotal = 0;

        for (const item of items) {
            const productId = Number(item.product_id);
            const quantity = Number(item.quantity);

            const product = await get(
                `SELECT
                    id,
                    product_name,
                    selling_price,
                    buying_price,
                    stock_quantity
                 FROM products
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND business_id = ?`,
                [productId, businessId]
            );

            if (!product) {
                throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
            }

            const sellingPrice = Number(product.selling_price);
            const buyingPrice = Number(product.buying_price);
            const availableStock = Number(product.stock_quantity);

            if (
                !Number.isFinite(sellingPrice) ||
                sellingPrice < 0 ||
                !Number.isFinite(buyingPrice) ||
                buyingPrice < 0
            ) {
                throw new Error("INVALID_PRODUCT_PRICE");
            }

            if (
                !Number.isFinite(availableStock) ||
                availableStock < quantity
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    `INSUFFICIENT_STOCK:${product.product_name}`
                );
            }

            const lineSubtotal = roundMoney(
                quantity * sellingPrice
            );

            subtotal = roundMoney(subtotal + lineSubtotal);

            saleItems.push({
                product_id: product.id,
                product_name: product.product_name,
                quantity,
                unit_price: sellingPrice,
                unit_cost: buyingPrice,
                line_subtotal: lineSubtotal
            });
        }

        if (requestedDiscount > subtotal) {
            throw new Error("DISCOUNT_EXCEEDS_SUBTOTAL");
        }

        const totalAmount = roundMoney(
            subtotal - requestedDiscount
        );

        if (totalAmount <= 0) {
            throw new Error("INVALID_SALE_TOTAL");
        }

        const amountPaid = roundMoney(
            normalizedPayments.reduce(
                (sum, payment) => sum + payment.amount,
                0
            )
        );

        if (amountPaid > totalAmount) {
            throw new Error("PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_TOTAL");
        }

        const balanceDue = roundMoney(
            totalAmount - amountPaid
        );

        if (balanceDue > 0 && !customerId) {
            throw new Error("CUSTOMER_REQUIRED_FOR_CREDIT");
        }

        let customer = null;
        let debtorTransactionId = null;

        if (customerId) {
            customer = await get(
                `SELECT
                    id,
                    full_name,
                    credit_limit,
                    credit_enabled
                 FROM customers
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND business_id = ?`,
                [customerId, businessId]
            );

            if (!customer) {
                throw new Error("CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND");
            }
        }

        if (balanceDue > 0) {
            if (Number(customer.credit_enabled) !== 1) {
                throw new Error("CUSTOMER_CREDIT_DISABLED");
            }

            await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customerId);

            const balances = await get(
                `SELECT
                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(total_amount)
                        FROM debtor_transactions
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND customer_id = ?
                    ), 0) AS total_debt,

                    COALESCE((
                        SELECT SUM(amount)
                        FROM debtor_payments
                        WHERE business_id = ?
                          AND customer_id = ?
                    ), 0) AS total_paid`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    customerId,
                    businessId,
                    customerId
                ]
            );

            const existingNetBalance = calculateDebtorBalance(
                balances.total_debt,
                balances.total_paid
            );

            const projectedBalance = Math.max(
                0,
                roundMoney(existingNetBalance + balanceDue)
            );

            const creditLimit = Number(customer.credit_limit) || 0;

            if (
                creditLimit > 0 &&
                projectedBalance > creditLimit
            ) {
                throw new Error("CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED");
            }

            const today = kenyaDateParts();

            const daily = await get(
                `SELECT id, status
                 FROM debtor_daily_notebooks
                 WHERE business_id = ?
                   AND customer_id = ?
                   AND notebook_date = ?`,
                [businessId, customerId, today.date]
            );

            if (daily?.status === "closed") {
                throw new Error("DAILY_NOTEBOOK_CLOSED");
            }

            const monthly = await get(
                `SELECT id, status
                 FROM debtor_monthly_notebooks
                 WHERE business_id = ?
                   AND customer_id = ?
                   AND year = ?
                   AND month = ?`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    customerId,
                    today.year,
                    today.month
                ]
            );

            if (monthly?.status === "closed") {
                throw new Error("MONTHLY_NOTEBOOK_CLOSED");
            }
        }

        let allocatedDiscount = 0;

        for (let index = 0; index < saleItems.length; index++) {
            const item = saleItems[index];

            let itemDiscount = 0;

            if (requestedDiscount > 0) {
                if (index === saleItems.length - 1) {
                    itemDiscount = roundMoney(
                        requestedDiscount - allocatedDiscount
                    );
                } else {
                    itemDiscount = roundMoney(
                        requestedDiscount *
                        item.line_subtotal /
                        subtotal
                    );

                    allocatedDiscount = roundMoney(
                        allocatedDiscount + itemDiscount
                    );
                }
            }

            item.discount_amount = itemDiscount;
            item.line_total = roundMoney(
                item.line_subtotal - itemDiscount
            );
        }

        const saleDate = kenyaDateParts().date;
        const saleNumber = createSaleNumber(saleDate);

        const paymentStatus = balanceDue === 0
            ? "paid"
            : amountPaid > 0
                ? "partially_paid"
                : "unpaid";

        const saleResult = await run(
            `INSERT INTO sales (
                business_id,
                customer_id,
                sale_number,
                sale_date,
                subtotal,
                discount_amount,
                total_amount,
                amount_paid,
                balance_due,
                payment_status,
                status,
                notes,
                created_by
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            [
                businessId,
                customerId,
                saleNumber,
                saleDate,
                subtotal,
                requestedDiscount,
                totalAmount,
                amountPaid,
                balanceDue,
                paymentStatus,
                "completed",
                notes,
                userId
            ]
        );

        const saleId = saleResult.lastID;

        for (const item of saleItems) {
            await run(
                `INSERT INTO sale_items (
                    sale_id,
                    product_id,
                    product_name,
                    quantity,
                    unit_price,
                    unit_cost,
                    line_subtotal,
                    discount_amount,
                    line_total
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    saleId,
                    item.product_id,
                    item.product_name,
                    item.quantity,
                    item.unit_price,
                    item.unit_cost,
                    item.line_subtotal,
                    item.discount_amount,
                    item.line_total
                ]
            );

            const stockUpdate = await run(
                `UPDATE products
                 SET stock_quantity = stock_quantity - ?
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND business_id = ?
                   AND stock_quantity >= ?`,
                [
                    item.quantity,
                    item.product_id,
                    businessId,
                    item.quantity
                ]
            );

            if (stockUpdate.changes !== 1) {
                throw new Error(
                    `INSUFFICIENT_STOCK:${item.product_name}`
                );
            }

            const quantityAfter = await get(
                `SELECT stock_quantity
                 FROM products
                 WHERE id = ?
                   AND business_id = ?`,
                [item.product_id, businessId]
            );

            if (!quantityAfter) {
                throw new Error("PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND");
            }

            const after = Number(quantityAfter.stock_quantity);
            const before = roundMoney(after + item.quantity);

            await run(
                `INSERT INTO stock_movements (
                    business_id,
                    product_id,
                    movement_type,
                    quantity_before,
                    quantity_changed,
                    quantity_after,
                    reason,
                    reference_type,
                    reference_id,
                    created_by
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    item.product_id,
                    "stock_out",
                    before,
                    -item.quantity,
                    after,
                    `POS sale ${saleNumber}`,
                    "sale",
                    saleId,
                    userId
                ]
            );
        }

        for (const payment of normalizedPayments) {
            await run(
                `INSERT INTO sale_payments (
                    business_id,
                    sale_id,
                    payment_date,
                    amount,
                    payment_method,
                    reference,
                    notes,
                    created_by
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    saleId,
                    saleDate,
                    payment.amount,
                    payment.method,
                    payment.reference,
                    payment.notes,
                    userId
                ]
            );
        }

        if (balanceDue > 0) {
            const transactionNumber = `SALE-${saleNumber}`;

            const debtorResult = await run(
                `INSERT INTO debtor_transactions (
                    business_id,
                    customer_id,
                    sale_id,
                    transaction_number,
                    transaction_date,
                    total_amount,
                    notes,
                    created_by
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                [
                    businessId,
                    customerId,
                    saleId,
                    transactionNumber,
                    saleDate,
                    balanceDue,
                    `Outstanding balance from POS sale ${saleNumber}`,
                    userId
                ]
            );

            debtorTransactionId = debtorResult.lastID;

            let allocatedDebt = 0;
            let remainingDebt = balanceDue;

            for (let index = 0; index < saleItems.length; index++) {
                const item = saleItems[index];

                let itemDebt = 0;

                if (index === saleItems.length - 1) {
                    itemDebt = roundMoney(remainingDebt);
                } else if (totalAmount > 0) {
                    itemDebt = roundMoney(
                        balanceDue *
                        item.line_total /
                        totalAmount
                    );

                    itemDebt = Math.min(
                        itemDebt,
                        roundMoney(remainingDebt)
                    );

                    allocatedDebt = roundMoney(
                        allocatedDebt + itemDebt
                    );

                    remainingDebt = roundMoney(
                        balanceDue - allocatedDebt
                    );
                }

                if (itemDebt <= 0) {
                    continue;
                }

                await run(
                    `INSERT INTO debtor_transaction_items (
                        transaction_id,
                        product_id,
                        product_name,
                        quantity,
                        unit_price,
                        line_total
                    )
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
                    [
                        debtorTransactionId,
                        item.product_id,
                        item.product_name,
                        item.quantity,
                        roundMoney(itemDebt / item.quantity),
                        itemDebt
                    ]
                );
            }

            await ensureCustomerNotebooks(businessId, customerId);
        }

        await run("COMMIT");
        transactionStarted = false;

        return res.status(201).json({
            success: true,
            message: balanceDue > 0
                ? "Sale recorded and outstanding balance added to Debtor Management."
                : "Sale recorded successfully.",
            sale: {
                id: saleId,
                sale_number: saleNumber,
                sale_date: saleDate,
                customer_id: customerId,
                customer_name: customer?.full_name || null,
                subtotal,
                discount_amount: requestedDiscount,
                total_amount: totalAmount,
                amount_paid: amountPaid,
                balance_due: balanceDue,
                payment_status: paymentStatus,
                status: "completed",
                debtor_transaction_id: debtorTransactionId
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (transactionStarted) {
            await rollbackTransaction();
        }

        if (error.message === "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "The selected customer does not belong to this business.",
                404
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "CUSTOMER_REQUIRED_FOR_CREDIT") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "Select or register a customer before completing a sale with an unpaid balance.",
                400
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "CUSTOMER_CREDIT_DISABLED") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "Credit is disabled for this customer. Enable credit in Debtor Management or collect full payment.",
                400
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "This sale would exceed the customer's credit limit.",
                409
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "DAILY_NOTEBOOK_CLOSED") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "Today's debtor notebook is closed. This credit sale cannot be added.",
                409
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "MONTHLY_NOTEBOOK_CLOSED") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "This month's debtor notebook is closed. This credit sale cannot be added.",
                409
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "One or more selected products could not be found.",
                404
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "INVALID_PRODUCT_PRICE") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "A product has an invalid selling price or buying price.",
                409
            );
        }

        if (error.message.startsWith("INSUFFICIENT_STOCK:")) {
            const productName = error.message
                .split(":")
                .slice(1)
                .join(":");

            return sendError(
                res,
                `Not enough stock available for ${productName}.`,
                409
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "DISCOUNT_EXCEEDS_SUBTOTAL") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "The discount cannot exceed the sale subtotal.",
                400
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_TOTAL") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "The amount paid cannot exceed the sale total.",
                400
            );
        }

        if (error.message === "INVALID_SALE_TOTAL") {
            return sendError(
                res,
                "The sale total must be greater than zero.",
                400
            );
        }

        console.error("Create sale error:", error);

        return sendError(
            res,
            "Unable to record this sale. The transaction was rolled back."
        );
    }
});

module.exports = router;

========== settingsRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");

const router = express.Router();
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

router.use(requireAuth);

// ========================================
// GET MY PROFILE
// ========================================

router.get("/profile", (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.session.user.id;

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            id,
            full_name,
            email,
            phone,
            role,
            created_at
         FROM users
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [userId],
        (error, user) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Profile loading error:", error.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load your profile."
                });
            }

            if (!user) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "User account not found."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                user
            });
        }
    );
});

// ========================================
// UPDATE MY PROFILE
// ========================================

router.put("/profile", (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.session.user.id;

    const {
        full_name,
        email,
        phone
    } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof full_name !== "string" ||
        !full_name.trim() ||
        typeof email !== "string" ||
        !email.trim()
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Full name and email are required."
        });
    }

    const cleanName = full_name.trim();
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cleanPhone = typeof phone === "string"
        ? phone.trim()
        : "";

    if (cleanName.length > 100) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Full name is too long."
        });
    }

    if (
        cleanEmail.length > 254 ||
        !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(cleanEmail)
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid email address."
        });
    }

    if (cleanPhone.length > 30) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Phone number is too long."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ? AND id != ?`,
        [cleanEmail, userId],
        (checkError, existingUser) => {
            if (checkError) {
                console.error("Email check error:", checkError.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify your email address."
                });
            }

            if (existingUser) {
                return res.status(409).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "This email address is already in use."
                });
            }

            db.run(
                `UPDATE users
                 SET full_name = ?,
                     email = ?,
                     phone = ?
                 WHERE id = ?`,
                [
                    cleanName,
                    cleanEmail,
                    cleanPhone || null,
                    userId
                ],
                function (updateError) {
                    if (updateError) {
                        console.error(
                            "Profile update error:",
                            updateError.message
                        );

                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not update your profile."
                        });
                    }

                    if (this.changes === 0) {
                        return res.status(404).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "User account not found."
                        });
                    }

                    req.session.user.full_name = cleanName;
                    req.session.user.email = cleanEmail;

                    res.json({
                        success: true,
                        message: "Your profile was updated successfully."
                    });
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// ========================================
// CHANGE PASSWORD
// ========================================

router.post("/password", (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.session.user.id;

    const {
        current_password,
        new_password
    } = req.body;

    if (
        typeof current_password !== "string" ||
        typeof new_password !== "string" ||
        !current_password ||
        !new_password
    ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter your current and new passwords."
        });
    }

    if (new_password.length < 12 || new_password.length > 128) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Your new password must contain 12 to 128 characters."
        });
    }

    db.get(
        `SELECT password_hash
         FROM users
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [userId],
        async (error, user) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Password lookup error:", error.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not verify your account."
                });
            }

            if (!user) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "User account not found."
                });
            }

            try {
                const passwordMatches = await bcrypt.compare(
                    current_password,
                    user.password_hash
                );

                if (!passwordMatches) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Your current password is incorrect."
                    });
                }

                const newHash = await bcrypt.hash(new_password, 12);

                db.run(
                    `UPDATE users
                     SET password_hash = ?
                     WHERE id = ?`,
                    [newHash, userId],
                    function (updateError) {
                        if (updateError) {
                            console.error(
                                "Password update error:",
                                updateError.message
                            );

                            return res.status(500).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "Could not change your password."
                            });
                        }

                        if (this.changes === 0) {
                            return res.status(404).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "User account not found."
                            });
                        }

                        res.json({
                            success: true,
                            message: "Your password was changed successfully."
                        });
                    }
                );
            } catch (passwordError) {
                console.error(
                    "Password processing error:",
                    passwordError.message
                );

                res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "A password processing error occurred."
                });
            }
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

========== subscriptionRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

const router = express.Router();

router.use(requireAuth);

// GET CURRENT BUSINESS SUBSCRIPTION
router.get("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.get(
        `SELECT
            business_id,
            plan_name,
            status,
            trial_started_at,
            trial_ends_at,
            current_period_start,
            current_period_end,
            CASE
                WHEN status = 'trial'
                     AND trial_ends_at IS NOT NULL
                     AND datetime(trial_ends_at) <= datetime('now')
                THEN 'expired'
                WHEN status = 'active'
                     AND current_period_end IS NOT NULL
                     AND datetime(current_period_end) <= datetime('now')
                THEN 'expired'
                ELSE status
            END AS current_status,
            CASE
                WHEN status = 'trial'
                     AND trial_ends_at IS NOT NULL
                THEN MAX(
                    0,
                    CAST(
                        (julianday(trial_ends_at) - julianday('now')) * 86400
                        AS INTEGER
                    )
                )
                WHEN status = 'active'
                     AND current_period_end IS NOT NULL
                THEN MAX(
                    0,
                    CAST(
                        (julianday(current_period_end) - julianday('now')) * 86400
                        AS INTEGER
                    )
                )
                ELSE 0
            END AS remaining_seconds
         FROM subscriptions
         WHERE business_id = ?`,
        [businessId],
        (error, subscription) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Subscription lookup error:", error.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not load subscription information."
                });
            }

            if (!subscription) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "No subscription record was found for this business."
                });
            }

            return res.json({
                success: true,
                subscription: {
                    business_id: subscription.business_id,
                    plan_name: subscription.plan_name,
                    status: subscription.current_status,
                    trial_started_at: subscription.trial_started_at,
                    trial_ends_at: subscription.trial_ends_at,
                    current_period_start: subscription.current_period_start,
                    current_period_end: subscription.current_period_end,
                    remaining_seconds: subscription.remaining_seconds
                }
            });
        }
    );
});

// START THE 7-DAY FREE TRIAL
router.post("/start-trial", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    db.run(
        `UPDATE subscriptions
         SET
            status = 'trial',
            trial_started_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            trial_ends_at = datetime('now', '+7 days')
         WHERE business_id = ?
           AND status = 'pending'
           AND trial_started_at IS NULL
           AND trial_ends_at IS NULL`,
        [businessId],
        function (error) {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Trial activation error:", error.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not start your free trial."
                });
            }

            if (this.changes === 0) {
                return db.get(
                    `SELECT status, trial_started_at, trial_ends_at
                     FROM subscriptions
                     WHERE business_id = ?`,
                    [businessId],
                    (lookupError, subscription) => {
                        if (lookupError) {
                            console.error(
                                "Trial status lookup error:",
                                lookupError.message
                            );

                            return res.status(500).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "Could not verify your trial status."
                            });
                        }

                        if (!subscription) {
                            return res.status(404).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "No subscription record was found."
                            });
                        }

                        return res.status(409).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Your trial cannot be started again.",
                            subscription
                        });
                    }
                );
            }

            return res.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Your 7-day WIN Pro free trial has started.",
                trial_duration_days: 7
            });
        }
    );
});

// LOAD COMPLETE UPGRADE REQUEST DETAILS
function returnUpgradeRequest(res, requestId, alreadyExists) {
    db.get(
        `SELECT
            sp.id AS request_id,
            sp.business_id,
            sp.user_id,
            sp.plan_name,
            sp.billing_cycle,
            sp.amount,
            sp.currency,
            sp.status AS payment_status,
            sp.submitted_at,

            u.full_name AS customer_name,
            u.email AS customer_email,

            b.business_name,
            b.business_type,
            b.email AS business_email,
            b.phone AS business_phone,
            b.country AS business_country

         FROM subscription_payments sp

         INNER JOIN users u
            ON sp.user_id = u.id

         INNER JOIN businesses b
            ON sp.business_id = b.id

         WHERE sp.id = ?
           AND sp.business_id = ?`,
        [requestId, reqBusinessId(res)],
        (error, request) => {
            if (error) {
                console.error(
                    "Upgrade request details error:",
                    error.message
                );

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "The request was saved, but its details could not be loaded."
                });
            }

            if (!request) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Upgrade request details could not be found."
                });
            }

            return res.status(alreadyExists ? 200 : 201).json({
                success: true,
                already_exists: alreadyExists,
                message: alreadyExists
                    ? "Your existing upgrade request is ready to continue on WhatsApp."
                    : "Your upgrade request has been saved. Continue on WhatsApp.",
                request
            });
        }
    );
}

// Keep the authenticated business ID available to the response helper.
function reqBusinessId(res) {
    return res.locals.businessId;
}

// CREATE OR REUSE WIN PRO UPGRADE REQUEST
router.post("/request", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const userId = req.session.user.id;
    const billingCycle = req.body.billing_cycle;

    const plans = {
        monthly: 1000,
        annual: 10000
    };

    if (!plans[billingCycle]) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please select a valid billing cycle."
        });
    }

    const amount = plans[billingCycle];

    res.locals.businessId = businessId;

    // Reuse an existing pending request for the same plan.
    db.get(
        `SELECT id
         FROM subscription_payments
         WHERE business_id = ?
           AND billing_cycle = ?
           AND status = 'pending'
         LIMIT 1`,
        [businessId, billingCycle],
        (lookupError, existingRequest) => {
            if (lookupError) {
                console.error(
                    "Upgrade request lookup error:",
                    lookupError.message
                );

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not check existing upgrade requests."
                });
            }

            if (existingRequest) {
                returnUpgradeRequest(
                    res,
                    existingRequest.id,
                    true
                );
                return;
            }

            db.run(
                `INSERT INTO subscription_payments (
                    business_id,
                    user_id,
                    plan_name,
                    billing_cycle,
                    amount,
                    currency,
                    status
                ) VALUES (?, ?, 'WIN Pro', ?, ?, 'KES', 'pending')`,
                [businessId, userId, billingCycle, amount],
                function (insertError) {
                    if (insertError) {
                        console.error(
                            "Upgrade request creation error:",
                            insertError.message
                        );

                        return res.status(500).json({
                            success: false,
                            message: "Could not save your upgrade request."
                        });
                    }

                    returnUpgradeRequest(
                        res,
                        this.lastID,
                        false
                    );
                }
            );
        }
    );
});

// SAVE A SUPPORT MESSAGE
router.post("/support", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const userId = req.session.user.id;

    const subject = String(req.body.subject || "").trim();
    const message = String(req.body.message || "").trim();

    if (!subject || !message) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please provide both a subject and a message."
        });
    }

    if (subject.length > 150 || message.length > 5000) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Your subject or message is too long."
        });
    }

    db.run(
        `INSERT INTO support_messages (
            business_id,
            user_id,
            subject,
            message,
            status
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'open')`,
        [businessId, userId, subject, message],
        function (error) {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Support message error:", error.message);

                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not send your support message."
                });
            }

            return res.status(201).json({
                success: true,
                message: "Your support message has been received.",
                message_id: this.lastID
            });
        }
    );
});

module.exports = router;

========== supplierRoutes.js ==========

const express = require("express");
const router = express.Router();
const db = require("../config/database");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/requireAuth");

router.use(requireAuth);

function validId(value) {
    const id = Number(value);
    return Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0 ? id : null;
}

function validDate(value) {
    return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) &&
        !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value + "T00:00:00Z"));
}

function validMonth(year, month) {
    return Number.isInteger(year) &&
        year >= 2000 &&
        Number.isInteger(month) &&
        month >= 1 &&
        month <= 12;
}

function getSupplier(businessId, supplierId, callback) {
    db.get(
        `SELECT * FROM suppliers WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [supplierId, businessId],
        callback
    );
}

function checkClosedMonth(businessId, supplierId, date, callback) {
    const year = Number(date.slice(0, 4));
    const month = Number(date.slice(5, 7));

    db.get(
        `SELECT id FROM supplier_month_closures
         WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?
         AND year = ? AND month = ?`,
        [businessId, supplierId, year, month],
        (error, closure) => {
            if (error) return callback(error);
            callback(null, Boolean(closure));
        }
    );
}

// GET ALL SUPPLIERS
router.get("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;

    const sql = `
        SELECT id, supplier_name, phone, email, notes, created_at
        FROM suppliers
        WHERE business_id = ?
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `;

    db.all(sql, [businessId], (error, suppliers) => {
        if (error) {
            console.error("Get suppliers error:", error.message);
            return res.status(500).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Could not load suppliers."
            });
        }

        res.json({ success: true, suppliers });
    });
});

// ADD SUPPLIER
router.post("/", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const supplierName = String(req.body.supplier_name || "").trim();
    const phone = String(req.body.phone || "").trim();
    const email = String(req.body.email || "").trim();
    const notes = String(req.body.notes || "").trim();

    if (!supplierName) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Supplier name is required."
        });
    }

    if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please enter a valid email address."
        });
    }

    db.run(
        `INSERT INTO suppliers
         (business_id, supplier_name, phone, email, notes)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [businessId, supplierName, phone || null, email || null, notes || null],
        function (error) {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Add supplier error:", error.message);
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not add supplier."
                });
            }

            res.status(201).json({
                success: true,
                message: "Supplier added successfully.",
                supplierId: this.lastID
            });
        }
    );
});

// UPDATE SUPPLIER
router.put("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const supplierId = validId(req.params.id);
    const supplierName = String(req.body.supplier_name || "").trim();
    const phone = String(req.body.phone || "").trim();
    const email = String(req.body.email || "").trim();
    const notes = String(req.body.notes || "").trim();

    if (!supplierId || !supplierName) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Valid supplier ID and supplier name are required."
        });
    }

    if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Please enter a valid email address."
        });
    }

    db.run(
        `UPDATE suppliers
         SET supplier_name = ?, phone = ?, email = ?, notes = ?
         WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
        [supplierName, phone || null, email || null, notes || null,
            supplierId, businessId],
        function (error) {
            if (error) {
                console.error("Update supplier error:", error.message);
                return res.status(500).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Could not update supplier."
                });
            }

            if (!this.changes) {
                return res.status(404).json({
                    success: false,
                    message: "Supplier not found."
                });
            }

            res.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Supplier updated successfully."
            });
        }
    );
});

// DELETE SUPPLIER â€” PROTECT FINANCIAL HISTORY
router.delete("/:id", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const supplierId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!supplierId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid supplier ID."
        });
    }

    getSupplier(businessId, supplierId, (error, supplier) => {
        if (error) {
            return res.status(500).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Could not check supplier."
            });
        }

        if (!supplier) {
            return res.status(404).json({
                success: false,
                message: "Supplier not found."
            });
        }

        db.get(
            `SELECT
                (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM purchases
                 WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?) +
                (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM supplier_payments
                 WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?) +
                (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM supplier_month_closures
                 WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?) AS history_count`,
            [businessId, supplierId, businessId, supplierId,
                businessId, supplierId],
            (historyError, result) => {
                if (historyError) {
                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Could not check supplier history."
                    });
                }

                if (Number(result.history_count) > 0) {
                    return res.status(409).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "This supplier has financial history and cannot be deleted."
                    });
                }

                db.run(
                    `DELETE FROM suppliers
                     WHERE id = ? AND business_id = ?`,
                    [supplierId, businessId],
                    function (deleteError) {
                        if (deleteError) {
                            return res.status(500).json({
                                success: false,
                                message: "Could not delete supplier."
                            });
                        }

                        res.json({
                            success: true,
                            message: "Supplier deleted successfully."
                        });
                    }
                );
            }
        );
    });
});

// SUPPLIER ACCOUNT OVERVIEW
router.get("/:id/overview", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const supplierId = validId(req.params.id);

    if (!supplierId) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Invalid supplier ID."
        });
    }

    getSupplier(businessId, supplierId, (error, supplier) => {
        if (error || !supplier) {
            return res.status(error ? 500 : 404).json({
                success: false,
                message: error ? "Could not load supplier." : "Supplier not found."
            });
        }

        db.get(
            `SELECT
                COALESCE((SELECT SUM(total_amount)
                    FROM purchases
                    WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?), 0)
                    AS total_purchases,

                COALESCE((SELECT SUM(amount)
                    FROM supplier_payments
                    WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?), 0)
                    AS total_payments,

                (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM purchases
                    WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?)
                    AS purchase_count,

                (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM supplier_payments
                    WHERE business_id = ? AND supplier_id = ?)
                    AS payment_count`,
            [businessId, supplierId, businessId, supplierId,
                businessId, supplierId, businessId, supplierId],
            (summaryError, summary) => {
                if (summaryError) {
                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
                        message: "Could not calculate supplier account."
                    });
                }

                const purchases = Number(summary.total_purchases);
                const payments = Number(summary.total_payments);

                res.json({
                    success: true,
                    supplier,
                    account: {
                        total_purchases: purchases,
                        total_payments: payments,
                        outstanding_balance: Math.max(purchases - payments, 0),
                        supplier_credit: Math.max(payments - purchases, 0),
                        purchase_count: summary.purchase_count,
                        payment_count: summary.payment_count
                    }
                });
            }
        );
    });
});

// RECORD SUPPLIER PAYMENT
router.post("/:id/payments", (req, res) => {
    const businessId = req.session.user.business_id;
    const supplierId = validId(req.params.id);
    const paymentDate = String(req.body.payment_date || "");
    const amount = Number(req.body.amount);
    const method = String(req.body.payment_method || "").trim();
    const reference = String(req.body.reference || "").trim();
    const notes = String(req.body.notes || "").trim();

    if (!supplierId || !validDate(paymentDate) ||
        !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            success: false,
            message: "Enter a valid supplier, payment date and positive amount."
        });
    }

    getSupplier(businessId, supplierId, (error, supplier) => {
        if (error || !supplier) {
            return res.status(error ? 500 : 404).json({
                success: false,
                message: error ? "Could not check supplier." : "Supplier not found."
            });
        }

        checkClosedMonth(
            businessId, supplierId, paymentDate,
            (closureError, closed) => {
                if (closureError) {
                    return res.status(500).json({
                        success: false,
